    result = deliver(adapter, item(payload('v1', 10), 99, 'reviewed'))
    assert result.error_code == 'downstream_stale_revision'
    assert adapter.read_back([old.key])[0].key == old.key


def test_new_source_still_requires_monotonic_delivery_revision(adapter):
    old = item(payload(), 3, 'reviewed')
    assert deliver(adapter, old).succeeded
    assert deliver(adapter, item(payload('v2', 20), 2)).error_code == 'downstream_stale_revision'


@pytest.mark.parametrize('stored_json', ['{broken', '[]', 'null'])
def test_sql_invalid_stored_json_cannot_bypass_review_guard(sqlite_adapter, stored_json):
    adapter = sqlite_adapter
    old = item(payload(), 2, 'reviewed')
    assert deliver(adapter, old).succeeded
    with adapter._connection_factory() as connection:
        connection.execute(text('UPDATE envelopes SET payload_json = :payload'),
                           {'payload': stored_json})
    assert deliver(adapter, item(payload('v2', 20), 3)).error_code == 'downstream_review_guard'


def test_sql_update_preserves_review_committed_after_precheck(sqlite_adapter, monkeypatch):
    adapter = sqlite_adapter
    assert deliver(adapter, item(payload(), 1)).succeeded
    reviewed = item(payload(), 2, 'reviewed')
    original_select = adapter._select_one
    injected = False

    def select_then_review(connection, business_key):
        nonlocal injected
        row = original_select(connection, business_key)
        if not injected:
            injected = True
            connection.execute(text('''UPDATE envelopes SET revision = 2,
                review_state = 'reviewed', payload_hash = :hash'''),
                {'hash': reviewed.key.payload_hash})
        return row

    monkeypatch.setattr(adapter, '_select_one', select_then_review)
    result = deliver(adapter, item(payload(source_id=15), 3))
    assert not result.succeeded
    assert adapter.read_back([reviewed.key])[0].key == reviewed.key

===== FILE backend/tests/test_source_revision_workflow.py =====
import json
from dataclasses import replace
from datetime import datetime, timedelta, timezone

import pytest
from sqlalchemy import select

from app.connector_delivery import FixtureDownstreamAdapter, deliver_outbox_batch
from app.incremental_routing import configure_policy, set_admission, fence_outbox_payload
from app.models import ConnectorContractVersion, ProductionRun, WorkflowVersion
from app.runtime_outbox import enqueue_runtime_outbox
from app.workflow_runtime import build_incremental_projection_outbox_request, WorkflowRuntimeError
from test_content_ingress_incremental_routing import _fixture, _close, _headers, _payload


@pytest.mark.parametrize("stale_prior", [False, True])
@pytest.mark.parametrize("gray", [True, False])
@pytest.mark.parametrize("legacy_payload", [False, True])
def test_new_source_auto_overwrites_old_review_but_same_source_and_stale_corrections_do_not(monkeypatch, gray, legacy_payload, stale_prior):
    engine, db, client, asset = _fixture()
    try:
        if gray:
            scope = dict(category_key="space_image", workflow_definition_id=db.scalar(select(WorkflowVersion)).workflow_definition_id)
            policy = configure_policy(db, **scope, expected_revision=0, mode="split", ratio_percent=100,
                ownership_contract="source-revision-fixture", reason="测试", actor="test")
            set_admission(db, **scope, expected_revision=policy["revision"], paused=False, reason="开始", actor="test")
            db.commit()
        def admit(number):
            payload = _payload(event_id=f"source-review-{number}", asset_id=asset.id)
            payload["payload"]["content_version"] = f"v{number}"
            payload["occurred_at"] = (datetime(2026, 8, 13, tzinfo=timezone.utc) + timedelta(days=number)).isoformat()
            response = client.post("/api/content-ingress/events", headers=_headers(monkeypatch), json=payload)
            assert response.status_code == 200, response.text
            run = db.scalar(select(ProductionRun).where(ProductionRun.run_key == response.json()["production_run_key"]))
            nodes = json.loads(run.snapshot_json)["workflow"]["manifest"]["nodes"]
            step = next(n["node_instance_id"] for n in nodes if n["kind"] == "downstream_projection")
            return run, step
        if stale_prior:
            admit(0)
            stale = _payload(event_id="stale-v2", asset_id=asset.id)
            stale["payload"]["content_version"] = "v2"
            stale["occurred_at"] = datetime(2026, 8, 12, tzinfo=timezone.utc).isoformat()
            assert client.post("/api/content-ingress/events", headers=_headers(monkeypatch), json=stale).status_code == 200
        old, step = admit(1)
        target = db.scalar(select(ConnectorContractVersion).where(ConnectorContractVersion.direction == "downstream"))
        adapter = FixtureDownstreamAdapter(schema_fingerprint=target.schema_fingerprint)
        reviewed = build_incremental_projection_outbox_request(db, old, step_key=step,
            output_manifest={"score": 90}, review_state="reviewed")
        if legacy_payload:
            payload = dict(reviewed.payload)
            payload.pop("source_revision", None)
            reviewed = replace(reviewed, payload=payload)
        old_event = enqueue_runtime_outbox(db, reviewed)
        assert deliver_outbox_batch(db, target, worker_id="test", adapter=adapter).delivered_event_ids == (old_event.id,)
        db.commit()
        with pytest.raises(WorkflowRuntimeError, match="人工纠偏"):
            build_incremental_projection_outbox_request(db, old, step_key=step, output_manifest={"score": 10})
        new, new_step = admit(2)
        automatic = build_incremental_projection_outbox_request(db, new, step_key=new_step, output_manifest={"score": 30})
        assert automatic.review_state == "auto"
        assert automatic.payload["source_revision"]["source_version"] == "v2"
        assert automatic.revision > reviewed.revision
        event = enqueue_runtime_outbox(db, automatic)
        assert deliver_outbox_batch(db, target, worker_id="test", adapter=adapter).delivered_event_ids == (event.id,)
        assert adapter.rows[0].key.review_state == "auto"
        with pytest.raises(WorkflowRuntimeError, match="源版本"):
            build_incremental_projection_outbox_request(db, old, step_key=step, output_manifest={"score": 100}, review_state="reviewed")
        tampered = dict(automatic.payload)
        tampered["source_revision"] = {**tampered["source_revision"], "first_event_id": 999999}
        with pytest.raises(WorkflowRuntimeError, match="源版本"):
            fence_outbox_payload(db, tampered)
    finally:
        _close(engine, db)

===== FILE backend/tests/test_space_zero_drift_replay.py =====
from __future__ import annotations

import hashlib
import json
from copy import deepcopy
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Any

import pytest

from app.dimension_schema_registry import (
    ACTIVE_V13_VERSION,
    HISTORICAL_DEFAULT_VERSION,
    space_schema_definition_for_version,
)
from app.scoring import calculate_corrected_score, calculate_score


FIXTURE_PATH = (
    Path(__file__).parent
    / "fixtures"
    / "space_zero_drift_v1.json"
)
SPACE_KEYS = (
    "composition_viewpoint",
    "lighting_atmosphere",
    "color_material",
    "spatial_design_furnishing",
    "visual_hierarchy",
    "detail_completion",
    "inspiration_reference",
    "presentation_integrity",
)
def _deep_merge(
    target: dict[str, Any],
    source: dict[str, Any],
) -> dict[str, Any]:
    for key, value in source.items():
        if isinstance(value, dict) and isinstance(
            target.get(key), dict
        ):
            _deep_merge(target[key], value)
        else:
            target[key] = deepcopy(value)
    return target


def _precheck(case: dict[str, Any]) -> dict[str, Any]:
    payload: dict[str, Any] = {
        "classification": {
            "scope_status": "in_scope",
            "primary_confidence": 0.95,
        },
        "image_quality": {
            "quality_severity": "normal",
            "confidence": 0.95,
            "evidence": [],
        },
        "media_form": {},
        "needs_review": False,
        "review_reasons": [],
    }
    return _deep_merge(
        payload,
        case.get("precheck_overrides") or {},
    )


def _aesthetic(
    case: dict[str, Any],
) -> dict[str, Any] | None:
    if "aesthetic" in case and case["aesthetic"] is None:
        return None
    grades = case["grades"]
    evidence_count = int(case.get("evidence_count", 3))
    payload: dict[str, Any] = {
        "dimensions": {
            key: {
                "grade": grade,
                "evidence": [
                    f"证据{index + 1}"
                    for index in range(evidence_count)
                ],
            }
            for key, grade in zip(
                SPACE_KEYS,
                grades,
                strict=True,
            )
        },
        "special_checks": {},
        "assessment_confidence": float(
            case.get("assessment_confidence", 0.95)
        ),
        "needs_review": False,
        "review_reasons": [],
    }
    profile = case.get("profile")
    if profile is not None:
        payload["scoring_profile"] = profile
    return _deep_merge(
        payload,
        case.get("aesthetic_overrides") or {},
    )


def _schema(case: dict[str, Any]) -> dict[str, Any]:
    version = (
        ACTIVE_V13_VERSION
        if case.get("profile") == "space_aesthetic_v1.3"
        else HISTORICAL_DEFAULT_VERSION
    )
    return space_schema_definition_for_version(version)


def _canonical_sha256(payload: dict[str, Any]) -> str:
    canonical = json.dumps(
        payload,
        ensure_ascii=False,
        sort_keys=True,
        separators=(",", ":"),
    ).encode("utf-8")
    return hashlib.sha256(canonical).hexdigest()


with FIXTURE_PATH.open(encoding="utf-8") as fixture_file:
    FIXTURE = json.load(fixture_file)


@pytest.mark.parametrize(
    "case",
    FIXTURE["cases"],
    ids=lambda case: case["name"],
)
def test_space_schema_matches_frozen_legacy_output(
    case: dict[str, Any],
) -> None:
    precheck = _precheck(case)
    aesthetic = _aesthetic(case)
    definition = _schema(case)
    corrections = case.get("corrections")

    if corrections is None:
        result = calculate_score(
            precheck,
            aesthetic,
            dimension_schema=definition,
        )
    else:
        result = calculate_corrected_score(
            precheck,
            aesthetic,
            corrections,
            dimension_schema=definition,
        )

    current_summary = {
        key: result.get(key)
        for key in case["expected_summary"]
    }
    assert current_summary == case["expected_summary"]
    assert _canonical_sha256(result) == case["expected_sha256"]


def test_zero_drift_fixture_is_bound_to_known_legacy_engine() -> None:
    assert FIXTURE["fixture_version"] == "space-zero-drift-v1"
    assert FIXTURE["legacy_source_commit"] == (
        "2cbb5947e9c76cecb4a8f38a5d50f18952f8390f"
    )
    assert FIXTURE["canonical_json"] == {
        "ensure_ascii": False,
        "sort_keys": True,
        "separators": [",", ":"],
    }
    assert len(FIXTURE["cases"]) == 15

===== FILE backend/tests/test_split_runtime_admission.py =====
import asyncio
import threading

import pytest

from app.adaptive_admission import AdmissionGate, Sample
from app.bounded_runtime import BoundedRuntime, ProviderWait


def test_repeated_local_work_never_blocks_other_provider_settlement():
    from app.bounded_runtime import LocalWork, drive_sync

    async def scenario():
        preparing, release = threading.Event(), threading.Event()
        provider_entered, provider_release = asyncio.Event(), asyncio.Event()
        def heavy():
            preparing.set()
            assert release.wait(3)
            return 9
        async def provider():
            provider_entered.set()
            await provider_release.wait()
            return 3
        def second_prepare():
            yield ProviderWait(lambda: asyncio.sleep(0), 2)
            return (yield LocalWork(heavy))
        def paid():
            return (yield ProviderWait(provider, 2))
        async with BoundedRuntime(slots=2, phase_workers=1,
                                  phase_admission=AdmissionGate(1)) as runtime:
            settling = asyncio.create_task(runtime.run(paid()))
            await provider_entered.wait()
            preparing_task = asyncio.create_task(runtime.run(second_prepare()))
            try:
                assert await asyncio.to_thread(preparing.wait, 1)
                provider_release.set()
                assert await asyncio.wait_for(settling, 1) == 3
            finally:
                release.set()
                await asyncio.gather(preparing_task, settling, return_exceptions=True)
            assert preparing_task.result() == 9
    asyncio.run(scenario())
    def sync():
        return (yield LocalWork(lambda: 11))
    assert drive_sync(sync()) == 11


def test_cancel_local_work_waits_for_thread_and_does_not_dispatch():
    from app.bounded_runtime import LocalWork
    async def scenario():
        entered, release = threading.Event(), threading.Event()
        facts = []
        def prepare():
            entered.set()
            assert release.wait(3)
            facts.append('prepared')
        async def provider():
            facts.append('incorrect-dispatch')
        def operation():
            try:
                yield LocalWork(prepare)
                yield ProviderWait(provider, 1)
            finally:
                facts.append('closed')
        async with BoundedRuntime(slots=1, phase_workers=1,
                                  phase_admission=AdmissionGate(1)) as runtime:
            task = asyncio.create_task(runtime.run(operation()))
            assert await asyncio.to_thread(entered.wait, 1)
            task.cancel()
            release.set()
            with pytest.raises(asyncio.CancelledError):
                await task
        assert facts == ['prepared', 'closed']
    asyncio.run(scenario())


def test_queued_local_work_renews_lease_and_lost_lease_prevents_preparation():
    from app.bounded_runtime import LocalWork
    async def scenario():
        gate = AdmissionGate(1)
        pulses, prepared = [], []
        def pulse():
            pulses.append('renew')
            if len(pulses) == 2:
                raise ValueError('lease lost')
        async with BoundedRuntime(slots=2, phase_workers=1, phase_admission=gate) as runtime:
            async with gate:
                with pytest.raises(ValueError, match='lease lost'):
                    await asyncio.wait_for(runtime.local_work(LocalWork(
                        lambda: prepared.append(True), heartbeat=pulse, heartbeat_interval=.01)), 1)
            assert runtime.provider_completed == runtime.provider_errors == 0
        assert pulses == ['renew', 'renew'] and prepared == []
    asyncio.run(scenario())


def test_provider_waits_do_not_hold_phase_gate_but_keep_hard_slots():
    async def scenario():
        gate = AdmissionGate(1)
        entered, release = asyncio.Event(), asyncio.Event()
        live = peak = prepared = 0

        async def provider():
            nonlocal live, peak
            live += 1
            peak = max(peak, live)
            if live == 4:
                entered.set()
            try:
                await release.wait()
                return 7
            finally:
                live -= 1

        def operation():
            nonlocal prepared
            prepared += 1
            value = yield ProviderWait(provider, 3)
            return value

        async with BoundedRuntime(slots=4, phase_workers=1, phase_admission=gate) as runtime:
            jobs = [asyncio.create_task(runtime.run(operation())) for _ in range(5)]
            try:
                await asyncio.wait_for(entered.wait(), 2)
                assert live == prepared == 4
                assert gate.active == 0
            finally:
                release.set()
                values = await asyncio.gather(*jobs)
        assert values == [7] * 5
        assert peak == 4 and live == gate.active == 0

    asyncio.run(scenario())


def test_completed_provider_settles_even_when_prepare_gate_is_occupied():
    async def scenario():
        gate = AdmissionGate(1)
        entered, release = asyncio.Event(), asyncio.Event()
        facts = []

        async def provider():
            entered.set()
            await release.wait()
            return 'ok'

        def operation():
            value = yield ProviderWait(provider, 2)
            facts.append(value)
            return value

        async with BoundedRuntime(slots=2, phase_workers=1, phase_admission=gate) as runtime:
            task = asyncio.create_task(runtime.run(operation()))
            await asyncio.wait_for(entered.wait(), 1)
            async with gate:
                release.set()
                assert await asyncio.wait_for(task, 1) == 'ok'
        assert facts == ['ok']

    asyncio.run(scenario())


def test_low_or_missing_memory_blocks_only_new_operations_and_recovers():
    from app.runtime_start_gate import MemoryStartGate

    async def scenario():
        guard = MemoryStartGate()
        entered, release = asyncio.Event(), asyncio.Event()
        started = []

        async def provider():
            entered.set()
            await release.wait()

        def operation(index):
            started.append(index)
            yield ProviderWait(provider, 3)
            return index

        async with BoundedRuntime(slots=2, phase_workers=1,
                                  phase_admission=AdmissionGate(1), start_gate=guard) as runtime:
            await guard.update(Sample(1, 0, 10, 4096, 0))
            first = asyncio.create_task(runtime.run(operation(1)))
            await asyncio.wait_for(entered.wait(), 1)
            await guard.update(Sample(1, 0, 10, 100, 0))
            second = asyncio.create_task(runtime.run(operation(2)))
            await asyncio.sleep(0)
            assert started == [1]
            release.set()
            assert await asyncio.wait_for(first, 1) == 1
            await guard.update(None)
            assert not guard.ready and not second.done()
            await guard.update(Sample(1, 0, 10, 4096, 0))
            assert await asyncio.wait_for(second, 1) == 2
        assert started == [1, 2]

    asyncio.run(scenario())


def test_split_cancellation_during_preparation_cannot_dispatch_or_leak():
    async def scenario():
        entered, release = threading.Event(), threading.Event()
        facts = []
        gate = AdmissionGate(1)

        async def provider():
            facts.append('incorrect-dispatch')

        def operation():
            try:
                entered.set()
                release.wait(2)
                yield ProviderWait(provider, 1)
            except Exception:
                facts.append('unknown-settled')
            finally:
                facts.append('closed')

        async with BoundedRuntime(slots=1, phase_workers=1, phase_admission=gate) as runtime:
            task = asyncio.create_task(runtime.run(operation()))
            while not entered.is_set():
                await asyncio.sleep(.001)
            task.cancel()
            release.set()
            with pytest.raises(asyncio.CancelledError):
                await task
            assert gate.active == 0
        assert facts == ['unknown-settled', 'closed']

    asyncio.run(scenario())


def test_split_mode_requires_explicit_validated_autotune_configuration(monkeypatch):
    from app.async_runtime_worker import configured_split_admission
    monkeypatch.delenv('WORKER_RUNTIME_SPLIT_ADMISSION', raising=False)
    assert configured_split_admission(slots=32, validated=32, autotune=True) is False
    monkeypatch.setenv('WORKER_RUNTIME_SPLIT_ADMISSION', '1')
    assert configured_split_admission(slots=32, validated=32, autotune=True) is True
    for validated, autotune in [(0, True), (33, True), (32, False)]:
        with pytest.raises(ValueError):
            configured_split_admission(slots=32, validated=validated, autotune=autotune)
    monkeypatch.setenv('WORKER_RUNTIME_SPLIT_ADMISSION', 'yes')
    with pytest.raises(ValueError):
        configured_split_admission(slots=32, validated=32, autotune=True)


def test_stale_memory_permission_expires_without_another_update(monkeypatch):
    import app.runtime_start_gate as module
    clock = [100.0]
    monkeypatch.setattr(module, 'monotonic', lambda: clock[0])
    guard = module.MemoryStartGate(max_age_seconds=45)
    asyncio.run(guard.update(Sample(1, 0, 10, 4096, 0)))
    assert guard.ready
    clock[0] = 146
    assert not guard.ready


def test_memory_rechecked_after_waiting_for_a_hard_slot():
    from app.runtime_start_gate import MemoryStartGate

    async def scenario():
        guard, entered, release = MemoryStartGate(), asyncio.Event(), asyncio.Event()
        started = []

        async def provider():
            entered.set()
            await release.wait()

        def operation(index):
            started.append(index)
            yield ProviderWait(provider, 3)

        async with BoundedRuntime(slots=1, phase_workers=1,
                phase_admission=AdmissionGate(1), start_gate=guard) as runtime:
            await guard.update(Sample(1, 0, 10, 4096, 0))
            first = asyncio.create_task(runtime.run(operation(1)))
            await entered.wait()
            second = asyncio.create_task(runtime.run(operation(2)))
            await asyncio.sleep(0)
            await guard.update(None)
            release.set()
            await first
            # Let the waiting operation acquire the released hard slot.
            await asyncio.sleep(.02)
            assert started == [1]
            await guard.update(Sample(1, 0, 10, 4096, 0))
            await second
        assert started == [1, 2]

    asyncio.run(scenario())

===== FILE backend/tests/test_sqlite_claim_transaction.py =====
import sqlite3

from sqlalchemy import create_engine
from sqlalchemy.exc import OperationalError
from sqlalchemy.orm import Session

from app.database import (
    is_transient_sqlite_claim_error,
    reserve_sqlite_immediate_transaction,
)


def test_reserve_sqlite_claim_skips_nested_begin() -> None:
    engine = create_engine("sqlite://")
    with Session(engine) as db:
        driver = db.connection().connection.driver_connection
        driver.execute("BEGIN")
        assert driver.in_transaction is True

        reserve_sqlite_immediate_transaction(db)

        assert driver.in_transaction is True


def test_reserve_sqlite_claim_starts_immediate_transaction() -> None:
    engine = create_engine("sqlite://")
    with Session(engine) as db:
        reserve_sqlite_immediate_transaction(db)

        driver = db.connection().connection.driver_connection
        assert driver.in_transaction is True


def test_nested_transaction_claim_error_is_transient() -> None:
    error = OperationalError(
        "BEGIN IMMEDIATE",
        {},
        sqlite3.OperationalError(
            "cannot start a transaction within a transaction"
        ),
    )

    assert is_transient_sqlite_claim_error(error) is True


def test_unrelated_sqlite_operational_error_is_not_transient() -> None:
    error = OperationalError(
        "SELECT missing",
        {},
        sqlite3.OperationalError("no such table: missing"),
    )

    assert is_transient_sqlite_claim_error(error) is False


def test_busy_timeout_is_configurable_and_defaults_to_thirty_seconds() -> None:
    """锁等待上限要能不改代码调整，默认值必须足够撑过真实回归的争用。

    2026-08-28 run133：250 条真实回归时锁等待实测 38-48 秒，而当时硬编码的
    10 秒上限直接抛 OperationalError，两次就熔断了整轮。
    """

    import os

    from app.database import _busy_timeout_ms

    os.environ.pop("SQLITE_BUSY_TIMEOUT_MS", None)
    assert _busy_timeout_ms() == 30_000

    try:
        os.environ["SQLITE_BUSY_TIMEOUT_MS"] = "45000"
        assert _busy_timeout_ms() == 45_000
        # 非法值一律回落默认，不能变成 0（那等于完全不等锁）。
        for bad in ("", "   ", "abc", "0", "-5"):
            os.environ["SQLITE_BUSY_TIMEOUT_MS"] = bad
            assert _busy_timeout_ms() == 30_000
    finally:
        os.environ.pop("SQLITE_BUSY_TIMEOUT_MS", None)

===== FILE backend/tests/test_sqlite_history_migration_cli.py =====
from __future__ import annotations

import importlib.util
import json
import sqlite3
import stat
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Any

import pytest


def _history_script() -> Any:
    script = (
        Path(__file__).resolve().parents[2]
        / "scripts"
        / "migrate-sqlite-history-to-mysql.py"
    )
    specification = importlib.util.spec_from_file_location(
        "labelsystem_sqlite_history_migration_script",
        script,
    )
    assert specification is not None
    assert specification.loader is not None
    module = importlib.util.module_from_spec(specification)
    specification.loader.exec_module(module)
    return module


def _active_source(path: Path) -> Path:
    connection = sqlite3.connect(path)
    try:
        connection.executescript(
            """
            PRAGMA journal_mode=WAL;
            PRAGMA foreign_keys=ON;
            CREATE TABLE events (
                id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY,
                value TEXT NOT NULL,
                updated_at TEXT NOT NULL
            );
            INSERT INTO events(id, value, updated_at)
            VALUES (1, 'first', '2026-08-31T08:00:00Z');
            """
        )
        connection.commit()
    finally:
        connection.close()
    return path


def test_snapshot_uses_online_backup_and_emits_private_consistent_manifest(
    tmp_path: Path,
) -> None:
    module = _history_script()
    source = _active_source(tmp_path / "active.db")
    snapshot = tmp_path / "receipts" / "history.db"
    manifest_path = tmp_path / "receipts" / "history.manifest.json"

    manifest = module.create_history_snapshot(
        source,
        snapshot,
        manifest_path,
    )

    payload = json.loads(manifest_path.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
    assert manifest["format"] == "label-system-history-snapshot-v1"
    assert payload == manifest
    assert payload["snapshot"]["integrity_check"] == "ok"
    assert payload["snapshot"]["foreign_key_violations"] == 0
    assert payload["snapshot"]["table_row_counts"] == {"events": 1}
    assert payload["snapshot"]["max_updated_timestamps"] == {
        "events.updated_at": "2026-08-31T08:00:00Z"
    }
    assert snapshot.stat().st_mode & 0o777 == stat.S_IRUSR
    assert manifest_path.stat().st_mode & 0o777 == stat.S_IRUSR
    with sqlite3.connect(f"file:{snapshot}?mode=ro", uri=True) as connection:
        assert connection.execute("PRAGMA integrity_check").fetchone()[0] == "ok"
        assert connection.execute("SELECT value FROM events").fetchone()[0] == "first"
    serialized = json.dumps(payload, ensure_ascii=False)
    assert "mysql+pymysql://" not in serialized
    assert "password" not in serialized.lower()


def test_import_input_rejects_a_direct_copy_without_a_matching_manifest(
    tmp_path: Path,
) -> None:
    module = _history_script()
    source = _active_source(tmp_path / "active.db")

    with pytest.raises(module.HistoryMigrationError, match="清单"):
        module.load_verified_history_snapshot(
            source,
            tmp_path / "missing.manifest.json",
        )


def test_post_snapshot_insert_invalidates_source_identity(
    tmp_path: Path,
) -> None:
    module = _history_script()
    source = _active_source(tmp_path / "active.db")
    snapshot = tmp_path / "history.db"
    manifest_path = tmp_path / "history.manifest.json"
    module.create_history_snapshot(source, snapshot, manifest_path)
    module.assert_source_unchanged(manifest_path)

    with sqlite3.connect(source) as connection:
        connection.execute(
            "INSERT INTO events(id, value, updated_at) VALUES (?, ?, ?)",
            (2, "second", "2026-08-31T08:05:00Z"),
        )
        connection.commit()

    with pytest.raises(
        module.HistoryMigrationError,
        match="source_changed_after_snapshot",
    ):
        module.assert_source_unchanged(manifest_path)


def test_tampered_snapshot_is_rejected_before_import(tmp_path: Path) -> None:
    module = _history_script()
    source = _active_source(tmp_path / "active.db")
    snapshot = tmp_path / "history.db"
    manifest_path = tmp_path / "history.manifest.json"
    module.create_history_snapshot(source, snapshot, manifest_path)
    snapshot.chmod(0o600)
    with snapshot.open("ab") as output:
        output.write(b"tampered")
    snapshot.chmod(0o400)

    with pytest.raises(module.HistoryMigrationError, match="大小|SHA-256"):
        module.load_verified_history_snapshot(snapshot, manifest_path)

===== FILE backend/tests/test_staged_reviews.py =====
from __future__ import annotations

import json
from datetime import timedelta

import pytest
from fastapi.testclient import TestClient
from sqlalchemy import create_engine, event, inspect as sa_inspect
from sqlalchemy.orm import Session
from sqlalchemy.pool import StaticPool

from app.database import Base, get_db
from app.main import app, current_user
from app.migrations import run_migrations
from app.models import (
    Asset,
    EvaluationJob,
    EvaluationResult,
    HumanReview,
    ModelConfig,
    PromptVersion,
    ReviewPanel,
    SamplingPolicy,
    User,
)
from app.regression import reviewed_truth_snapshot
from app.strategy_bundle import (
    build_evaluation_strategy_snapshot,
    get_or_create_bundle,
)


def _client_with_result(*, level: str = "L3", needs_review: bool = False):
    engine = create_engine(
        "sqlite://",
        connect_args={"check_same_thread": False},
        poolclass=StaticPool,
    )
    Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
    with engine.begin() as connection:
        run_migrations(connection)
    db = Session(engine, expire_on_commit=False)
    user = User(is_admin=True, role="admin",
        username="review-owner",
        password_hash="unused",
        display_name="审核员",
    )
    asset = Asset(
        original_name="review.jpg",
        stored_name="review.jpg",
        mime_type="image/jpeg",
        size_bytes=10,
        sha256="b" * 64,
    )
    prompt = PromptVersion(
        stage="A",
        name="单提示词",
        version="review-A1",
        system_prompt="system prompt for staged review",
        user_prompt="user prompt",
        rubric_version="R1",
        status="published",
    )
    model = ModelConfig(
        name="review model",
        model_id="review-model",
        base_url="https://example.test/v1",
        api_path="/chat/completions",
    )
    policy = SamplingPolicy(id=1, revision=1)
    db.add_all([user, asset, prompt, model, policy])
    db.flush()
    bundle = get_or_create_bundle(
        db=db,
        model_config=model,
        prompt_a=prompt,
        prompt_b=None,
        rubric_version="R1",
        engine_version="E1",
        risk_review_version=None,
        sampling_policy=policy,
    )
    job = EvaluationJob(
        asset_id=asset.id,
        prompt_a_id=prompt.id,
        strategy_bundle_id=bundle.id,
        status="completed",
        stage="done",
        progress=100,
    )
    db.add(job)
    db.flush()
    result = EvaluationResult(
        asset_id=asset.id,
        job_id=job.id,
        strategy_bundle_id=bundle.id,
        strategy_snapshot_json=build_evaluation_strategy_snapshot(
            db=db,
            bundle=bundle,
            prompt_a=prompt,
            prompt_b=None,
            sampling_policy=policy,
            aesthetic={"scoring_profile": "space_aesthetic_v1.3"},
        ),
        precheck_json=json.dumps(
            {
                "classification": {
                    "scope_status": "in_scope",
                    "primary_category": "住宅设计",
                },
                "image_quality": {"quality_severity": "normal"},
            }
        ),
        aesthetic_json=json.dumps(
            {
                "dimensions": {
                    "color_material": {"grade": 4},
                },
                "decision_rules": {},
            }
        ),
        scoring_json=json.dumps({"formal": True, "level": level}),
        raw_response_a="{}",
        score=75,
        level=level,
        confidence=0.95,
        needs_review=needs_review,
        model_id="review-model",
        prompt_a_version=prompt.version,
        prompt_b_version=None,
        rubric_version="R1",
        engine_version="E1",
    )
    db.add(result)
    db.commit()

    def override_db():
        yield db

    app.dependency_overrides[get_db] = override_db
    app.dependency_overrides[current_user] = lambda: user
    return engine, db, TestClient(app), result


def _close(engine, db) -> None:
    app.dependency_overrides.clear()
    db.close()
    engine.dispose()


def _review_payload(
    *,
    stage: str,
    revision: int,
    decision: str = "approved",
) -> dict:
    return {
        "reviewer_name": f"{stage}-reviewer",
        "decision": decision,
        "expected_stage": stage,
        "expected_review_revision": revision,
        "note": f"{stage} conclusion",
        "corrections": [],
    }


def _seed_legacy_initial_review(
    db: Session,
    result: EvaluationResult,
    *,
    decision: str = "approved",
) -> HumanReview:
    review = HumanReview(
        evaluation_id=result.id,
        stage="initial",
        reviewer_name="legacy-initial-reviewer",
        decision=decision,
        note="legacy initial conclusion",
        corrections_json="[]",
    )
    db.add(review)
    result.review_stage = "secondary"
    result.review_revision = 1
    db.commit()
    return review


def test_new_initial_review_requires_panel_and_keeps_legacy_history_empty() -> None:
    engine, db, client, result = _client_with_result()
    try:
        response = client.post(
            f"/api/evaluations/{result.id}/review",
            json=_review_payload(stage="initial", revision=0),
        )
        assert response.status_code == 409
        assert response.json()["detail"]["code"] == "REVIEW_PANEL_REQUIRED"
        detail = client.get(f"/api/evaluations/{result.id}").json()["evaluation"]
        assert detail["review_stage"] == "initial"
        assert detail["review_revision"] == 0
        assert detail["review_truth_status"] == "provisional"
        assert detail["review_history"] == []
        legacy_operation = client.get("/openapi.json").json()["paths"][
            "/api/evaluations/{evaluation_id}/review"
        ]["post"]
        assert legacy_operation["deprecated"] is True
        assert legacy_operation["summary"] == "兼容历史样本二审与仲裁"
    finally:
        _close(engine, db)


def test_legacy_secondary_agreement_completes_and_stale_is_409() -> None:
    engine, db, client, result = _client_with_result(
        level="L4", needs_review=True
    )
    try:
        _seed_legacy_initial_review(db, result)
        provisional = client.get(
            f"/api/evaluations/{result.id}"
        ).json()["evaluation"]
        assert provisional["review_truth_status"] == "provisional"
        db.refresh(result)
        with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="未经人工确认"):
            reviewed_truth_snapshot(result, "stable_control")

        stale = client.post(
            f"/api/evaluations/{result.id}/review",
            json=_review_payload(stage="secondary", revision=0),
        )
        assert stale.status_code == 409
        secondary = client.post(
            f"/api/evaluations/{result.id}/review",
            json=_review_payload(stage="secondary", revision=1),
        )
        assert secondary.status_code == 200, secondary.text
        assert secondary.json()["review_stage"] == "completed"
        assert db.query(HumanReview).filter_by(evaluation_id=result.id).count() == 2
        assert db.query(HumanReview).filter_by(
            evaluation_id=result.id,
            stage="secondary",
        ).one().reviewer_name == "review-owner"
    finally:
        _close(engine, db)


def test_disagreement_routes_to_arbitration_and_arbitration_completes() -> None:
    engine, db, client, result = _client_with_result(
        level="L5", needs_review=True
    )
    try:
        _seed_legacy_initial_review(db, result)
        second = client.post(
            f"/api/evaluations/{result.id}/review",
            json=_review_payload(
                stage="secondary", revision=1, decision="rejected"
            ),
        )
        assert second.status_code == 200, second.text
        assert second.json()["review_stage"] == "arbitration"
        arbitration = client.post(
            f"/api/evaluations/{result.id}/review",
            json=_review_payload(stage="arbitration", revision=2),
        )
        assert arbitration.status_code == 200, arbitration.text
        assert arbitration.json()["review_stage"] == "completed"
        assert arbitration.json()["review_revision"] == 3
        history = client.get(
            f"/api/evaluations/{result.id}"
        ).json()["evaluation"]["review_history"]
        assert [item["stage"] for item in history] == [
            "initial",
            "secondary",
            "arbitration",
        ]
    finally:
        _close(engine, db)


def test_legacy_arbitration_rejects_missing_historical_chain() -> None:
    engine, db, client, result = _client_with_result(
        level="L5", needs_review=True
    )
    try:
        result.review_stage = "arbitration"
        result.review_revision = 2
        db.commit()
        response = client.post(
            f"/api/evaluations/{result.id}/review",
            json=_review_payload(stage="arbitration", revision=2),
        )
        assert response.status_code == 409
        assert response.json()["detail"] == (
            "缺少历史初审或二审记录，不能执行兼容仲裁"
        )
        db.refresh(result)
        assert result.review_stage == "arbitration"
        assert result.review_revision == 2
        assert db.query(HumanReview).filter_by(
            evaluation_id=result.id
        ).count() == 0
    finally:
        _close(engine, db)


def _clone_evaluation(
    db: Session, source: EvaluationResult, *, created_at
) -> EvaluationResult:
    """克隆一条 evaluation 用于构造取数窗口场景。

    直接按列复制，避免重复 fixture 里那一大段初始化，也不会因为模型新增必填字段而失效。
    """
    # job_id 上有唯一约束，克隆必须各自配一个新 job。
    # 这里照 fixture 建最小 job：整列复制会把 (root_job_id, technical_attempt)
    # 这类复合唯一约束的值一起带过来，导致 INSERT 撞 UNIQUE。
    source_job = db.get(EvaluationJob, source.job_id)
    assert source_job is not None
    job = EvaluationJob(
        asset_id=source_job.asset_id,
        prompt_a_id=source_job.prompt_a_id,
        strategy_bundle_id=source_job.strategy_bundle_id,
        status=source_job.status,
        stage=source_job.stage,
        progress=source_job.progress,
    )
    db.add(job)
    db.flush()

    data = {
        column.key: getattr(source, column.key)
        for column in sa_inspect(EvaluationResult).columns
        if column.key not in {"id", "created_at", "job_id"}
    }
    clone = EvaluationResult(**data, job_id=job.id, created_at=created_at)
    db.add(clone)
    db.flush()
    return clone


def test_evaluations_scope_review_keeps_panel_samples_in_window() -> None:
    """待复核样本不能被更新的样本挤出取数窗口。

    复核工作台四个视图都要求样本带 panel 或已 completed；而本端点单页上限 1000 条、
    按创建时间倒序返回。样本总量超过窗口时，不带 scope=review 就会让全部待复核样本
    取不到，工作台四项计数全为 0、运营一条都点不进去。
    """
    engine, db, client, result = _client_with_result(level="L3", needs_review=True)
    try:
        base_created_at = result.created_at
        db.add(ReviewPanel(evaluation_id=result.id))
        db.flush()

        # 之后又产生了一批更新的样本，都没有进入复核范围
        newer_ids = [
            _clone_evaluation(
                db, result, created_at=base_created_at + timedelta(minutes=offset)
            ).id
            for offset in range(1, 4)
        ]
        db.commit()

        # 复现原始缺陷：窗口只放得下 2 条时，待复核样本被挤出去
        unscoped = client.get("/api/evaluations?limit=2")
        assert unscoped.status_code == 200
        unscoped_ids = {item["evaluation"]["id"] for item in unscoped.json()["items"]}
        assert result.id not in unscoped_ids

        # 带 scope=review 后必须取得到，且不夹带无 panel 的新样本
        scoped = client.get("/api/evaluations?limit=2&scope=review")
        assert scoped.status_code == 200
        payload = scoped.json()
        scoped_ids = {item["evaluation"]["id"] for item in payload["items"]}
        assert result.id in scoped_ids
        assert scoped_ids.isdisjoint(newer_ids)
        # total 也必须跟着过滤，否则前端分页会按错误总数计算
        assert payload["total"] == 1
    finally:
        _close(engine, db)


def test_evaluations_scope_review_includes_completed_without_panel() -> None:
    """已完成复核但没有 panel 的历史样本也属于复核范围。"""
    engine, db, client, result = _client_with_result(level="L3", needs_review=True)
    try:
        result.review_stage = "completed"
        db.flush()
        newer = _clone_evaluation(
            db, result, created_at=result.created_at + timedelta(minutes=5)
        )
        newer.review_stage = "initial"
        db.commit()

        scoped = client.get("/api/evaluations?limit=50&scope=review")
        assert scoped.status_code == 200
        payload = scoped.json()
        scoped_ids = {item["evaluation"]["id"] for item in payload["items"]}
        assert result.id in scoped_ids
        assert newer.id not in scoped_ids
        assert payload["total"] == 1
    finally:
        _close(engine, db)


def test_evaluations_without_scope_is_unfiltered() -> None:
    """不传 scope 时行为不变，避免影响其他调用方。"""
    engine, db, client, result = _client_with_result(level="L3", needs_review=True)
    try:
        newer = _clone_evaluation(
            db, result, created_at=result.created_at + timedelta(minutes=5)
        )
        db.commit()

        plain = client.get("/api/evaluations?limit=50")
        assert plain.status_code == 200
        payload = plain.json()
        ids = {item["evaluation"]["id"] for item in payload["items"]}
        assert {result.id, newer.id} <= ids
        assert payload["total"] == 2
    finally:
        _close(engine, db)


def test_evaluation_sampling_history_query_excludes_large_result_payloads() -> None:
    """分页纠偏不能为了抽样决策重新读取全库的大字段。"""
    engine, db, client, result = _client_with_result(level="L3", needs_review=True)
    statements: list[str] = []

    def capture_statement(
        _connection,
        _cursor,
        statement: str,
        _parameters,
        _context,
        _executemany,
    ) -> None:
        statements.append(statement)

    event.listen(engine, "before_cursor_execute", capture_statement)
    try:
        _clone_evaluation(
            db,
            result,
            created_at=result.created_at + timedelta(minutes=1),
        )
        db.commit()

        response = client.get("/api/evaluations?limit=1")
        assert response.status_code == 200, response.text

        history_queries = [
            statement
            for statement in statements
            if "ORDER BY evaluation_results.created_at ASC" in statement
        ]
        assert history_queries, statements
        history_query = history_queries[-1]
        for heavy_column in (
            "evaluation_results.raw_response_a",
            "evaluation_results.raw_response_b",
            "evaluation_results.normalized_result_json",
            "evaluation_results.strategy_snapshot_json",
        ):
            assert heavy_column not in history_query
    finally:
        event.remove(engine, "before_cursor_execute", capture_statement)
        _close(engine, db)

===== FILE backend/tests/test_stock_export_api.py =====
from sqlalchemy import select
from tests.test_employee_access import context
from app.models import Asset,BaselineSet,BaselineSetItem
from app.cover_regression import create_round
from app.cover_gateway import CoverParameters
from app.config import get_settings
from app.module_api import owner

def test_real_cookie_export_is_read_access_and_remains_stock_only(context,monkeypatch):
    sessions,login,_=context
    monkeypatch.setenv('STOCK_EXPORT_ENABLED','1')
    with sessions() as db:
        asset=db.scalar(select(Asset).where(Asset.category_key=='model_3d_su'))
        b=BaselineSet(name='export-api',category_key=asset.category_key,default_expected_level='L1',fingerprint='a'*64)
        db.add(b);db.flush()
        db.add(BaselineSetItem(baseline_set_id=b.id,asset_id=asset.id,asset_snapshot_json='{}'));db.flush()
        create_round(db,baseline_set_id=b.id,prompt='synthetic only',parameters=CoverParameters(),actor='fixture',settings=get_settings(),request_id='export-api-round');db.commit()
    viewer,_=login('viewer',[])
    response=viewer.post('/api/stock-exports',json={'source_kind':'cover','run_id':'export-api-round','format':'json'})
    assert response.status_code==200,response.text
    job=response.json();assert job['status']=='queued' and job['total']==1 and not job['download_url']
    assert viewer.get(f'/api/stock-exports/{job["id"]}/file').status_code==409
    assert viewer.post(f'/api/stock-exports/{job["id"]}/rebuild',json={'format':'xlsx'}).status_code==200
    assert viewer.post('/api/stock-exports',json={'source_kind':'cover','run_id':'export-api-round','incremental_enabled':True}).status_code==422
    assert viewer.post('/api/stock-exports',headers={'Origin':'https://attacker.invalid'},json={'source_kind':'cover','run_id':'export-api-round'}).status_code==403
    monkeypatch.delenv('STOCK_EXPORT_ENABLED')
    assert viewer.post('/api/stock-exports',json={'source_kind':'cover','run_id':'export-api-round'}).status_code==503
    assert owner('/api/stock-exports/anything/file')=='stock'

===== FILE backend/tests/test_stock_exports.py =====
import hashlib,io,json,zipfile
from datetime import datetime,timezone,timedelta
from sqlalchemy import select
import pytest
from tests.test_cover_regression import setup,make
from app.cover_regression import CoverRegressionItem,review_item,regenerate_item
from app.cover_attempts import CoverGenerationAttempt
from app.stock_exports import (freeze_results,create_export,execute_export,expire_packages,
    StockExportItem,StockExportSnapshot,StockExportJob,ExportError)


def ready(db,settings,set_id):
    run=make(db,settings,set_id)
    item=db.scalar(select(CoverRegressionItem))
    a=db.scalar(select(CoverGenerationAttempt));a.state='succeeded'
    image=next((settings.data_dir/'stock-images'/'inputs').iterdir()).read_bytes()
    digest=hashlib.sha256(image).hexdigest()
    target=settings.data_dir/'stock-images'/'outputs'/(digest+'.png')
    target.parent.mkdir(parents=True);target.write_bytes(image)
    a.result_json=json.dumps({'sha256':digest,'width':8,'height':8,'size_bytes':len(image)})
    a.gateway_unsettled=False;db.commit()
    return run,item,a,digest


def test_snapshot_freezes_human_and_raw_versions_and_rebuilds_after_expiry(setup):
    db,settings,sid=setup;run,item,old,digest=ready(db,settings,sid)
    review_item(db,item_id=item.id,attempt_id=old.id,expected_revision=0,verdict='adopt',reason='human',actor='tester');db.commit()
    newer=regenerate_item(db,item_id=item.id,input_directory=settings.data_dir/'stock-images'/'inputs')
    newer.state='succeeded';newer.result_json='{"sha256":"'+ 'e'*64 +'"}';db.commit()
    snap=freeze_results(db,source_kind='cover',run_id=run.id,selection={},result_mode='effective',actor='tester')
    db.commit();frozen=db.scalar(select(StockExportItem).where(StockExportItem.snapshot_id==snap.id))
    doc=json.loads(frozen.document_json)
    assert doc['human']['revision']==1 and doc['effective']['attempt_id']==old.id
    assert doc['raw']['attempt_id']==newer.id and doc['effective']['sha256']==digest
    before=frozen.document_json
    review_item(db,item_id=item.id,attempt_id=newer.id,expected_revision=1,verdict='uncertain',reason='changed later',actor='tester');db.commit()
    job=create_export(db,snapshot_id=snap.id,format='json',actor='tester');db.commit()
    execute_export(db,job.id,settings=settings)
    assert job.status=='succeeded'
    data=json.loads((settings.data_dir/'stock-exports'/job.file_name).read_text())
    assert data['items'][0]==doc and frozen.document_json==before
    job.expires_at=(datetime.now(timezone.utc)-timedelta(seconds=1)).isoformat();db.commit()
    assert expire_packages(db,settings=settings)==1
    assert db.get(StockExportSnapshot,snap.id) and db.get(StockExportItem,frozen.id)
    rebuilt=create_export(db,snapshot_id=snap.id,format='json',actor='tester');db.commit()
    execute_export(db,rebuilt.id,settings=settings)
    assert json.loads((settings.data_dir/'stock-exports'/rebuilt.file_name).read_text())==data


def test_all_pages_filters_and_missing_images_are_explicit(setup):
    db,settings,sid=setup;run,item,a,digest=ready(db,settings,sid)
    for i in range(2005):
        # Snapshot writer has no old 2000-row cap or page-dependent input.
        db.add(CoverRegressionItem(id=f'additional-{i}',run_id=run.id,baseline_item_id=100+i,
            asset_id=item.asset_id,original_name=f'pending-{i}.png',input_snapshot_json='{}'))
    db.commit()
    all_rows=freeze_results(db,source_kind='cover',run_id=run.id,selection={},result_mode='raw',actor='tester');db.commit()
    assert all_rows.total==2006
    snap=freeze_results(db,source_kind='cover',run_id=run.id,selection={'status':'succeeded'},result_mode='effective',actor='tester');db.commit()
    assert snap.total==1
    job=create_export(db,snapshot_id=snap.id,format='images_zip',actor='tester');db.commit()
    (settings.data_dir/'stock-images'/'outputs'/(digest+'.png')).unlink()
    execute_export(db,job.id,settings=settings)
    assert job.status=='succeeded' and job.missing_files==1
    with zipfile.ZipFile(settings.data_dir/'stock-exports'/job.file_name) as z:
        assert 'results.json' in z.namelist() and json.loads(z.read('files.json'))[0]['error']=='image_missing'


def test_export_restart_preserves_same_snapshot_and_other_packages(setup):
    db,settings,sid=setup;run,item,a,_=ready(db,settings,sid)
    snap=freeze_results(db,source_kind='cover',run_id=run.id,selection={},result_mode='raw',actor='tester');db.commit()
    first=create_export(db,snapshot_id=snap.id,format='json',actor='tester');db.commit();execute_export(db,first.id,settings=settings)
    original=(settings.data_dir/'stock-exports'/first.file_name).read_bytes()
    second=create_export(db,snapshot_id=snap.id,format='xlsx',actor='tester');db.commit()
    second.status='running';db.commit() # interrupted worker's persisted job
    execute_export(db,second.id,settings=settings)
    assert second.status=='succeeded' and (settings.data_dir/'stock-exports'/first.file_name).read_bytes()==original
    with zipfile.ZipFile(settings.data_dir/'stock-exports'/second.file_name) as z:
        import xml.etree.ElementTree as ET
        for name in z.namelist():
            if name.endswith('.xml') or name.endswith('.rels'):ET.fromstring(z.read(name))
        text=z.read('xl/worksheets/sheet2.xml').decode()
        assert 'effective' in text and 'raw' in text


def test_rejects_unknown_filter_and_mode_and_wrong_format(setup):
    db,settings,sid=setup;run,_,_,_=ready(db,settings,sid)
    with pytest.raises(ExportError,match='unknown_filter'):
        freeze_results(db,source_kind='cover',run_id=run.id,selection={'page':1},result_mode='raw',actor='tester')
    with pytest.raises(ExportError,match='invalid_result_mode'):
        freeze_results(db,source_kind='cover',run_id=run.id,selection={},result_mode='whatever',actor='tester')


def test_baseline_reference_is_separate_from_actual_human_correction(setup):
    from app.models import BaselineRegressionRun,BaselineRegressionItem,BaselineSetItem,EvaluationResult
    db,settings,sid=setup
    sample=db.scalar(select(BaselineSetItem))
    run=BaselineRegressionRun(baseline_set_id=sid,sequence_no=1,category_key='space_image',
        strategy_snapshot_json='{}',execution_snapshot_json='{}',baseline_set_fingerprint='b'*64,status='completed',total=1)
    db.add(run);db.flush()
    evaluation=EvaluationResult(asset_id=sample.asset_id,job_id=999,precheck_json='{}',scoring_json='{}',
        raw_response_a='{"level":"L4"}',level='L1',score=95,review_revision=1,
        model_id='fixture',prompt_a_version=1,prompt_b_version=1,rubric_version='fixture',engine_version='fixture',
        correction_history_json='[{"corrector":"tester","old_value":"L4","new_value":"L1"}]')
    db.add(evaluation);db.flush()
    item=BaselineRegressionItem(run_id=run.id,baseline_set_item_id=sample.id,asset_id=sample.asset_id,
        evaluation_id=evaluation.id,status='completed',expected_level='L2',expected_result_json='{"level":"L2"}',
        result_snapshot_json='{"predicted_level":"L4","authoritative_score":30}')
    db.add(item);db.commit()
    snap=freeze_results(db,source_kind='baseline',run_id=str(run.id),selection={},result_mode='effective',actor='tester');db.commit()
    doc=next(__import__('app.stock_exports',fromlist=['documents']).documents(db,snap.id))
    assert doc['raw']['level']=='L4' and doc['effective']['level']=='L1'
    assert doc['reference']['expected_level']=='L2' and doc['human']['revision']==1
    raw=freeze_results(db,source_kind='baseline',run_id=str(run.id),selection={},result_mode='raw',actor='tester');db.commit()
    assert next(__import__('app.stock_exports',fromlist=['documents']).documents(db,raw.id))['effective']['level']=='L4'


def test_xlsx_long_json_round_trips_control_characters_and_formula_prefix(tmp_path):
    from app.stock_export_xlsx import write_xlsx
    import xml.etree.ElementTree as ET
    doc={'item_id':'=formula','name':'=SUM(1,2)','status':'completed','human':None,
         'raw':{'title':'🪑'*18000+'\x01'+ '='*35000},'effective':{'title':'x'*50000}}
    path=tmp_path/'long.xlsx';write_xlsx(path,lambda:iter([doc]))
    with zipfile.ZipFile(path) as z:
        root=ET.fromstring(z.read('xl/worksheets/sheet2.xml'))
        rows=root.findall('{*}sheetData/{*}row')[1:]
        texts=[''.join(row.findall('{*}c')[-1].itertext()) for row in rows]
        assert json.loads(''.join(texts))==doc
        assert all(len(t.encode('utf-16-le'))<=60000 for t in texts)
        assert b'<f>' not in z.read('xl/worksheets/sheet1.xml')


def test_zip_contains_the_frozen_image_and_manifest(setup):
    db,settings,sid=setup;run,item,a,digest=ready(db,settings,sid)
    snap=freeze_results(db,source_kind='cover',run_id=run.id,selection={},result_mode='effective',actor='tester');db.commit()
    job=create_export(db,snapshot_id=snap.id,format='images_zip',actor='tester');db.commit();execute_export(db,job.id,settings=settings)
    assert job.status=='succeeded' and job.missing_files==0
    with zipfile.ZipFile(settings.data_dir/'stock-exports'/job.file_name) as z:
        manifest=json.loads(z.read('files.json'))
        assert hashlib.sha256(z.read(manifest[0]['file'])).hexdigest()==digest


def test_low_disk_fails_without_deleting_core_images_or_other_packages(setup,monkeypatch):
    from types import SimpleNamespace
    db,settings,sid=setup;run,item,a,digest=ready(db,settings,sid)
    snap=freeze_results(db,source_kind='cover',run_id=run.id,selection={},result_mode='raw',actor='tester');db.commit()
    job=create_export(db,snapshot_id=snap.id,format='images_zip',actor='tester');db.commit()
    monkeypatch.setattr('shutil.disk_usage',lambda path:SimpleNamespace(free=0))
    execute_export(db,job.id,settings=settings)
    assert job.status=='failed' and job.error_code=='insufficient_export_space'
    assert (settings.data_dir/'stock-images'/'outputs'/(digest+'.png')).is_file()
    assert not list((settings.data_dir/'stock-exports').glob('*.part'))

===== FILE backend/tests/test_stock_lab.py =====
"""HTTP contracts against isolated SQLite; no provider or business writes."""
from copy import deepcopy
import json

from fastapi.testclient import TestClient
from sqlalchemy import select

from test_prompt_lab import lab


def test_stock_api_drafts_samples_truth_and_production_boundary(lab):
    c = lab.client
    root = "/api/prompt-lab"
    assert c.get(root + "/catalog").status_code == 200
    source = c.get(f"{root}/mechanisms/{lab.ids.workflow}/manifest").json()
    body = {"source_version_id": source["source_version_id"], "expected_source_hash": source["source_hash"],
            "expected_revision": 0, "manifest": source["manifest"], "note": "stock only"}
    url = root + "/drafts/test-stock"
    assert TestClient(c.app).put(url, json=body).status_code == 401
    r = c.put(url, json=body)
    assert r.status_code == 200, r.text
    assert r.json()["revision"] == 1
    assert c.put(url, json=body).json() == r.json()
    conflict = deepcopy(body); conflict["note"] = "conflict"
    assert c.put(url, json=conflict).status_code == 409
    assert c.get(url).json()["manifest"] == source["manifest"]
    frozen = c.post(url + "/freeze", json={"revision": 1})
    assert frozen.status_code == 201, frozen.text
    assert frozen.json()["production_binding_changed"] is False
    assert c.post(url + "/freeze", json={"revision": 1}).json() == frozen.json()
    assert c.get(f"{root}/mechanisms/{lab.ids.workflow}/manifest").json() == source
    bad = deepcopy(body); bad["expected_revision"] = 1; bad["manifest"]["category_key"] = "other"
    assert c.put(url, json=bad).status_code == 422
    assert c.post(url + "/freeze", json={"revision": 1, "activate": True}).status_code == 422
    for path in ["/incremental-controls", "/incremental-mechanisms", "/production/activate"]:
        assert c.post(root + path, json={}).status_code == 404

    clone = {"source_set_id": lab.ids.baseline, "item_ids": lab.ids.items,
             "name": "stock clone", "request_id": "clone-0001"}
    result = c.post(root + "/baseline-sets/clone", json=clone)
    assert result.status_code == 201, result.text
    assert c.post(root + "/baseline-sets/clone", json=clone).json() == result.json()
    target = result.json()["baseline_set_id"]
    page = c.get(f"{root}/baseline-sets/{target}/items?limit=1").json()
    assert len(page["items"]) == 1 and page["next_offset"] == 1
    item = page["items"][0]
    truth_url = f"{root}/baseline-sets/{target}/items/{item['item_id']}/truth"
    updated = deepcopy(item["expected_result"]); updated["color"] = "白色"
    payload = {"expected_hash": item["canonical_hash"], "expected_result": updated,
               "reason": "caller confirmed", "request_id": "truth-0001", "confirmed": False}
    assert c.post(truth_url, json=payload).status_code == 422
    payload["confirmed"] = True
    result = c.post(truth_url, json=payload)
    assert result.status_code == 200, result.text
    assert result.json()["confirmation_source"] == "api_client_assertion"
    assert c.post(truth_url, json=payload).json() == result.json()
    payload["request_id"] = "truth-0002"
    assert c.post(truth_url, json=payload).status_code == 409
    assert c.get(truth_url).json()["expected_result"]["color"] == "白色"
    original = c.get(f"{root}/baseline-sets/{lab.ids.baseline}/items/{lab.ids.items[0]}/truth").json()
    assert original["expected_result"]["color"] == "gray"


def test_stock_control_checks_identity_and_preserves_success(lab):
    from app.models import BaselineRegressionItem, ProductionRun, ProductionStepAttempt
    c = lab.client; root = "/api/prompt-lab"
    r = c.post(root + "/runs", json={"workflow_version_id": lab.ids.workflow, "baseline_set_id": lab.ids.baseline})
    assert r.status_code == 201, r.text
    run_id = r.json()["id"]
    with lab.sessions() as db:
        items = db.scalars(select(BaselineRegressionItem).where(BaselineRegressionItem.run_id == run_id)).all()
        ids = [item.id for item in items]
    endpoint = f"{root}/runs/{run_id}/control"
    payload = {"item_ids": ids, "action": "pause", "request_id": "pause-0001"}
    with lab.sessions() as db:
        item = db.get(BaselineRegressionItem, ids[-1])
        attempt = db.scalar(select(ProductionStepAttempt).where(ProductionStepAttempt.run_id == item.production_run_id))
        old_status = attempt.status; attempt_id = attempt.id
        attempt.status = "running"; db.commit()
    busy = c.post(endpoint, json=payload)
    assert busy.status_code == 409 and busy.json()["detail"]["code"] == "stock_item_in_flight"
    with lab.sessions() as db:
        first = db.get(BaselineRegressionItem, ids[0])
        assert db.get(ProductionRun, first.production_run_id).status != "paused"
        db.get(ProductionStepAttempt, attempt_id).status = old_status; db.commit()
    r = c.post(endpoint, json=payload)
    assert r.status_code == 200, r.text
    assert all(v["status"] == "paused" for v in r.json()["items"])
    assert c.post(endpoint, json=payload).json() == r.json()
    r = c.post(endpoint, json={**payload, "action": "resume", "request_id": "resume-0001"})
    assert r.status_code == 200, r.text
    lab.drain()
    assert c.get(f"{root}/runs/{run_id}").json()["status"] == "completed"
    r = c.post(endpoint, json={**payload, "action": "retry", "request_id": "retry-0001"})
    assert r.status_code == 409
    with lab.sessions() as db:
        item = db.get(BaselineRegressionItem, ids[0])
        child = db.get(ProductionRun, item.production_run_id)
        child.source_type = "content_ingress_event"
        db.commit()
    r = c.post(endpoint, json={**payload, "action": "cancel", "request_id": "cancel-0001"})
    assert r.status_code == 409 and r.json()["detail"]["code"] == "stock_scope_mismatch"


def test_component_and_import_are_isolated_atomic_and_idempotent(lab):
    from app.models import WorkflowNodeAsset, BaselineSet
    c = lab.client; root = "/api/prompt-lab"
    with lab.sessions() as db:
        asset = db.scalar(select(WorkflowNodeAsset).where(WorkflowNodeAsset.category_key == "knowledge_graph", WorkflowNodeAsset.kind == "prompt"))
        asset_id = asset.id
    source = c.get(f"{root}/components/{asset_id}").json()
    # Existing seeded assets may have an empty working copy; use frozen prompt as source content.
    mechanism = c.get(f"{root}/mechanisms/{lab.ids.workflow}").json()
    prompt = mechanism["prompt_nodes"][0]
    content = {"stage": "stock_test", "system_prompt": prompt["system_prompt"] + "\nstock test",
               "user_prompt": prompt["user_prompt"], "rubric_version": "test-stock"}
    body = {"source_asset_id": asset_id, "content": content, "request_id": "component-0001"}
    r = c.post(root + "/components", json=body)
    assert r.status_code == 201, r.text
    assert c.post(root + "/components", json=body).json() == r.json()
    independent = c.post(root + "/components", json={**body, "request_id": "component-0002"})
    assert independent.status_code == 201, independent.text
    assert independent.json()["asset_key"] != r.json()["asset_key"]
    assert c.get(f"{root}/components/{asset_id}").json() == source
    item = {"asset_id": lab.ids.other_asset, "expected_level": "L2"}
    body = {"category_key": "model_3d_su", "name": "import test", "items": [item], "request_id": "import-0001"}
    r = c.post(root + "/baseline-sets/import", json=body)
    assert r.status_code == 201, r.text
    assert c.post(root + "/baseline-sets/import", json=body).json() == r.json()
    invalid = {**body, "name": "bad import", "request_id": "import-0002", "items": [item, {"asset_id": 999999, "expected_level": "L1"}]}
    assert c.post(root + "/baseline-sets/import", json=invalid).status_code == 422
    with lab.sessions() as db:
        assert db.scalar(select(BaselineSet.id).where(BaselineSet.name == "bad import")) is None

===== FILE backend/tests/test_stock_mechanism_selection.py =====
from __future__ import annotations

import hashlib
import json
from types import SimpleNamespace

import pytest
from fastapi.testclient import TestClient
from sqlalchemy import create_engine, func, select
from sqlalchemy.orm import Session
from sqlalchemy.pool import StaticPool

from app.audit import canonical_json
from app.evaluation_component_catalog import canonical_component_hash
from app.baseline_regression import (
    StockMechanismError,
    resolve_stock_mechanism_selection,
)
from app.database import Base, get_db
from app.main import _baseline_run_selection, app, current_user
from app.models import (
    Asset,
    BaselineRegressionRun,
    BaselineSet,
    BaselineSetItem,
    EvaluationJob,
    ModelConfig,
    ModelRegistryEntry,
    PromptVersion,
    ProductionRun,
    User,
    WorkflowDefinition,
    WorkflowVersion,
)
from tests.v3_contract_fixtures import add_active_v3_contract


def _mechanism(db: Session, *, category_key: str = "space_image") -> WorkflowVersion:
    definition = WorkflowDefinition(
        workflow_key=f"{category_key}.stock",
        name="存量回归完整评测机制",
        description="",
        owner="platform",
        allowed_categories_json=canonical_json([category_key]),
        status="active",
        created_by="ops",
    )
    db.add(definition)
    db.flush()
    prompt_content = {
        "stage": "C",
        "system_prompt": "提取知识图谱字段",
        "user_prompt": "返回结构化结果",
        "rubric_version": "rubric-v1",
    }
    nodes = [
        {
            "node_instance_id": "upstream_1",
            "kind": "upstream_connector",
            "display_name": "读取上游数据",
            "config": {},
            "input_schema": {"type": "object"},
            "output_schema": {"type": "object"},
            "error_policy": {"kind": "fail"},
            "retry_policy": {"kind": "fixed", "max_attempts": 1},
            "evidence_fields": ["request_id"],
        },
        {
            "node_instance_id": "prompt_c",
            "kind": "prompt",
            "display_name": "知识图谱字段提取",
            "config": {
                "asset_key": "prompt_c",
                "prompt_version_id": 31,
                "model_registry_id": 7,
                "max_tokens": 8192,
                "thinking_level": "high",
                "component_snapshot": {
                    "kind": "prompt",
                    "version_id": 31,
                    "version": "component-v2",
                    "content_hash": canonical_component_hash(prompt_content),
                    "content": prompt_content,
                },
                "model_snapshot": {
                    "model_registry_id": 7,
                    "name": "主评测模型",
                    "model_id": "fixture-model",
                },
            },
            "input_schema": {"type": "object"},
            "output_schema": {"type": "object"},
            "error_policy": {"kind": "fail"},
            "retry_policy": {"kind": "fixed", "max_attempts": 1},
            "evidence_fields": ["request_id", "knowledge_graph_fields"],
        },
        {
            "node_instance_id": "redline_1",
            "kind": "rule_module",
            "display_name": "准入与红线",
            "config": {
                "asset_key": "eligibility_redline",
                "module_type_key": "eligibility_redline",
                "rule_module_version_id": 41,
                "input_bindings": {"prompt": "prompt_c.output"},
                "scope": "stock",
                "component_snapshot": {
                    "kind": "rule_module",
                    "version_id": 41,
                    "version": "3",
                    "module_type_key": "eligibility_redline",
                    "content_hash": "b" * 64,
                    "content_json": "{}",
                },
            },
            "input_schema": {"type": "object"},
            "output_schema": {"type": "object"},
            "error_policy": {"kind": "fail"},
            "retry_policy": {"kind": "fixed", "max_attempts": 1},
            "evidence_fields": ["rule_hits"],
        },
        {
            "node_instance_id": "downstream_1",
            "kind": "downstream_projection",
            "display_name": "写入下游",
            "config": {},
            "input_schema": {"type": "object"},
            "output_schema": {"type": "object"},
            "error_policy": {"kind": "fail"},
            "retry_policy": {"kind": "fixed", "max_attempts": 1},
            "evidence_fields": ["request_id"],
        },
    ]
    manifest = {
        "schema_version": "workflow-v3",
        "category_key": category_key,
        "lane_key": "stock_regression",
        "run_modes": ["stock_regression"],
        "nodes": nodes,
        "edges": [
            {"from": "upstream_1", "to": "prompt_c"},
            {"from": "prompt_c", "to": "redline_1"},
            {"from": "redline_1", "to": "downstream_1"},
        ],
        "queue_class": "validation",
        "input_schema": {"type": "object"},
        "output_schema": {"type": "object"},
        "resource_policy": {"max_parallel": 1},
    }
    digest = hashlib.sha256(canonical_json(manifest).encode()).hexdigest()
    version = WorkflowVersion(
        workflow_definition_id=definition.id,
        version="3",
        status="active",
        workflow_schema_version="workflow-v3",
        step_manifest_json=canonical_json(nodes),
        edge_manifest_json=canonical_json(manifest["edges"]),
        input_schema_json=canonical_json(manifest["input_schema"]),
        output_schema_json=canonical_json(manifest["output_schema"]),
        resource_policy_json=canonical_json(
            {
                "max_parallel": 1,
                "queue_class": "validation",
                "category_key": category_key,
                "lane_key": "stock_regression",
                "run_modes": ["stock_regression"],
            }
        ),
        canonical_hash=digest,
        validation_report_json=canonical_json({"ok": True, "errors": []}),
        created_by="ops",
    )
    db.add(version)
    db.flush()
    return version


def test_stock_selection_freezes_one_complete_v3_mechanism() -> None:
    engine = create_engine("sqlite:///:memory:")
    Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
    try:
        with Session(engine) as db:
            version = _mechanism(db)

            selected = resolve_stock_mechanism_selection(
                db,
                category_key="space_image",
                workflow_version_id=version.id,
            )

            assert selected.version.id == version.id
            assert selected.snapshot_hash
            frozen = selected.snapshot["workflow"]["manifest"]
            assert frozen["schema_version"] == "workflow-v3"
            assert frozen["nodes"][1]["config"]["component_snapshot"][
                "content_hash"
            ] == canonical_component_hash(
                {
                    "stage": "C",
                    "system_prompt": "提取知识图谱字段",
                    "user_prompt": "返回结构化结果",
                    "rubric_version": "rubric-v1",
                }
            )
            assert frozen["nodes"][1]["config"]["model_snapshot"]["model_id"] == "fixture-model"
            assert frozen["nodes"][2]["config"]["component_snapshot"]["content_hash"] == "b" * 64
            assert [item["node_instance_id"] for item in selected.components] == [
                "upstream_1",
                "prompt_c",
                "redline_1",
                "downstream_1",
            ]
    finally:
        engine.dispose()


@pytest.mark.parametrize(
    ("mutate", "code"),
    [
        (lambda version: setattr(version, "workflow_schema_version", "workflow-v2"), "stock_mechanism_schema_invalid"),
        (lambda version: setattr(version, "status", "blocked"), "stock_mechanism_unavailable"),
    ],
)
def test_stock_selection_rejects_incomplete_or_unavailable_versions(mutate, code: str) -> None:
    engine = create_engine("sqlite:///:memory:")
    Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
    try:
        with Session(engine) as db:
            version = _mechanism(db)
            mutate(version)
            db.flush()

            with pytest.raises(StockMechanismError) as exc_info:
                resolve_stock_mechanism_selection(
                    db,
                    category_key="space_image",
                    workflow_version_id=version.id,
                )
            assert exc_info.value.code == code
    finally:
        engine.dispose()


def test_stock_selection_rejects_other_category_and_missing_run_mode() -> None:
    engine = create_engine("sqlite:///:memory:")
    Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
    try:
        with Session(engine) as db:
            version = _mechanism(db)
            with pytest.raises(StockMechanismError) as wrong_category:
                resolve_stock_mechanism_selection(
                    db,
                    category_key="material_image",
                    workflow_version_id=version.id,
                )
            assert wrong_category.value.code == "stock_mechanism_category_mismatch"

            policy = json.loads(version.resource_policy_json)
            policy["run_modes"] = ["incremental"]
            version.resource_policy_json = canonical_json(policy)
            db.flush()
            with pytest.raises(StockMechanismError) as wrong_mode:
                resolve_stock_mechanism_selection(
                    db,
                    category_key="space_image",
                    workflow_version_id=version.id,
                )
            assert wrong_mode.value.code == "stock_mechanism_run_mode_invalid"
    finally:
        engine.dispose()


def test_stock_legacy_execution_projects_selected_module_contents() -> None:
    from app.baseline_regression import prepare_stock_legacy_execution
    from app.evaluation_component_catalog import MODULE_TYPE_KEYS
    from app.evaluation_mechanism_migration import split_legacy_revision
    from app.strategy_bundle import build_model_config_snapshot

    engine = create_engine("sqlite:///:memory:")
    Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
    try:
        with Session(engine) as db:
            source_model = ModelConfig(
                name="兼容执行模型",
                provider="fixture",
                base_url="https://fixture.invalid",
                api_path="/dry-run",
                model_id="fixture-model",
                max_tokens=4096,
                thinking_mode="medium",
                active=True,
            )
            prompt_a = PromptVersion(
                category_key="space_image",
                pipeline_scope="shared",
                stage="A",
                name="提示词 A",
                version="a-v2",
                system_prompt="A",
                user_prompt="",
                rubric_version="rubric-v1",
                status="published",
                created_by="ops",
            )
            prompt_b = PromptVersion(
                category_key="space_image",
                pipeline_scope="shared",
                stage="B",
                name="提示词 B",
                version="b-v3",
                system_prompt="B",
                user_prompt="",
                rubric_version="rubric-v1",
                status="published",
                created_by="ops",
            )
            db.add_all([source_model, prompt_a, prompt_b])
            db.flush()
            registry = ModelRegistryEntry(
                role="main",
                name=source_model.name,
                provider=source_model.provider,
                protocol=source_model.protocol,
                base_url=source_model.base_url,
                api_path=source_model.api_path,
                model_id=source_model.model_id,
                max_tokens=source_model.max_tokens,
                thinking_mode=source_model.thinking_mode,
                structured_output=True,
                active=True,
                source_model_config_id=source_model.id,
                created_by="ops",
            )
            db.add(registry)
            db.flush()
            frozen_model_runtime = build_model_config_snapshot(source_model)
            legacy = add_active_v3_contract(db)
            base_bundle = {
                "category_key": "space_image",
                "revision": 1,
                "display_name": "旧执行合同",
                "contract_hash": "a" * 64,
                "contract": legacy["contract"],
                "classification_map": legacy["classification_map"],
                "subcategory_dimensions": legacy["subcategory_dimensions"],
                "dimension_deduction_rules": {},
                "media_penalty_enabled": True,
            }
            slices = split_legacy_revision(base_bundle)
            selected = {key: dict(slices[key]) for key in MODULE_TYPE_KEYS}
            selected["eligibility_redline"]["hit_score_cap"] = 37

            def prompt_node(prompt: PromptVersion) -> dict:
                return {
                    "node_instance_id": f"prompt_{prompt.stage.lower()}",
                    "kind": "prompt",
                    "display_name": f"提示词 {prompt.stage}",
                    "config": {
                        "prompt_version_id": prompt.id,
                        "model_registry_id": registry.id,
                        "max_tokens": 2048,
                        "thinking_level": "high",
                        "thinking_enabled": True,
                        "component_snapshot": {
                            "content": {"stage": prompt.stage},
                        },
                        "model_snapshot": {
                            "model_registry_id": registry.id,
                            "source_model_config_id": source_model.id,
                            "name": registry.name,
                            "provider": registry.provider,
                            "protocol": registry.protocol,
                            "model_id": registry.model_id,
                            "runtime_config": frozen_model_runtime,
                        },
                    },
                }

            def module_node(key: str, index: int) -> dict:
                return {
                    "node_instance_id": f"{key}_{index}",
                    "kind": "rule_module",
                    "display_name": key,
                    "config": {
                        "module_type_key": key,
                        "component_snapshot": {
                            "content_json": canonical_json(selected[key]),
                        },
                    },
                }

            evaluation_nodes = [prompt_node(prompt_a)]
            evaluation_nodes.extend(
                [
                    module_node("eligibility_redline", 1),
                    module_node("track_routing", 2),
                    prompt_node(prompt_b),
                ]
            )
            evaluation_nodes.extend(
                module_node(key, index)
                for index, key in enumerate(MODULE_TYPE_KEYS[2:], start=3)
            )
            boundaryless_manifest = {
                "category_key": "space_image",
                "nodes": evaluation_nodes,
                "edges": [
                    {
                        "from": source["node_instance_id"],
                        "to": target["node_instance_id"],
                    }
                    for source, target in zip(
                        evaluation_nodes, evaluation_nodes[1:]
                    )
                ],
            }
            with pytest.raises(StockMechanismError, match="读取上游"):
                prepare_stock_legacy_execution(
                    db,
                    manifest=boundaryless_manifest,
                    base_bundle=base_bundle,
                )
            nodes = [
                {
                    "node_instance_id": "upstream",
                    "kind": "upstream_connector",
                    "display_name": "读取上游数据",
                    "config": {"connector_key": "logical.space_image.input"},
                },
                *evaluation_nodes,
                {
                    "node_instance_id": "downstream",
