                    "kind": "downstream_projection",
                    "display_name": "写入下游",
                    "config": {"connector_key": "logical.space_image.output"},
                },
            ]
            manifest = {
                "category_key": "space_image",
                "resource_policy": {
                    "legacy_projection_snapshot": {
                        "schema_version": "legacy-projection-workflow-fields-v1",
                        "workflow_fields": slices.workflow_fields,
                    }
                },
                "nodes": nodes,
                "edges": [
                    {
                        "from": source["node_instance_id"],
                        "to": target["node_instance_id"],
                    }
                    for source, target in zip(nodes, nodes[1:])
                ],
            }
            source_model.model_id = "drifted-live-model"
            source_model.base_url = "https://drifted.invalid"
            registry.provider = "doubao"
            registry.thinking_mode = "disabled"
            base_bundle["display_name"] = "已漂移的当前合同"
            base_bundle["contract"]["spec_version"] = "drifted-spec"
            db.flush()

            execution = prepare_stock_legacy_execution(
                db, manifest=manifest, base_bundle=base_bundle
            )

            assert execution.prompt_a.id == prompt_a.id
            assert execution.prompt_b is not None
            assert execution.prompt_b.id == prompt_b.id
            assert execution.model_config.id == source_model.id
            assert execution.model_config.model_id == "fixture-model"
            assert execution.model_config.base_url == "https://fixture.invalid"
            assert execution.model_config.max_tokens == 2048
            assert execution.model_config.thinking_mode == "auto"
            assert execution.model_config.reasoning_effort == "high"
            from app.doubao import DoubaoClient

            client = object.__new__(DoubaoClient)
            client.config = execution.model_config
            request_payload: dict[str, object] = {}
            _, trace_reasoning, trace_thinking = client._generation_options(
                request_payload,
                output_budget=None,
                reasoning_effort=None,
                structured_output=False,
            )
            assert request_payload["reasoning_effort"] == "high"
            assert trace_reasoning == "high"
            assert trace_thinking == "auto"
            assert execution.v3_bundle["contract"]["redline_policy"][
                "hit_score_cap"
            ] == 37
            assert execution.v3_bundle["display_name"] == "旧执行合同"
            assert execution.v3_bundle["contract"]["spec_version"] != (
                "drifted-spec"
            )

            for item in manifest["nodes"]:
                if item["kind"] == "prompt":
                    item["config"].pop("thinking_enabled")
            legacy_default = prepare_stock_legacy_execution(
                db, manifest=manifest, base_bundle=base_bundle
            )
            assert legacy_default.model_config.thinking_mode == "disabled"
            assert legacy_default.model_config.reasoning_effort is None
    finally:
        engine.dispose()


def test_baseline_run_requires_complete_mechanism_and_rejects_component_overrides() -> None:
    engine = create_engine(
        "sqlite://",
        connect_args={"check_same_thread": False},
        poolclass=StaticPool,
    )
    Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
    db = Session(engine, expire_on_commit=False)
    try:
        user = User(
            username="ops",
            password_hash="unused",
            display_name="运营",
            is_admin=True,
        )
        asset = Asset(
            original_name="stock.jpg",
            stored_name="stock.jpg",
            mime_type="image/jpeg",
            size_bytes=10,
            sha256="c" * 64,
            category_key="space_image",
            status="uploaded",
        )
        db.add_all([user, asset])
        db.flush()
        baseline = BaselineSet(
            category_key="space_image",
            name="完整机制选择测试",
            description="",
            default_expected_level="L1",
            fingerprint="d" * 64,
            created_by="ops",
        )
        db.add(baseline)
        db.flush()
        db.add(
            BaselineSetItem(
                baseline_set_id=baseline.id,
                asset_id=asset.id,
                expected_level="L1",
                asset_snapshot_json=canonical_json(
                    {"asset_id": asset.id, "sha256": asset.sha256}
                ),
            )
        )
        version = _mechanism(db)
        db.add_all(
            [
                ModelConfig(
                    name="兼容执行模型",
                    provider="fixture",
                    base_url="https://fixture.invalid",
                    api_path="/dry-run",
                    model_id="fixture-model",
                    active=True,
                ),
                PromptVersion(
                    id=31,
                    category_key="space_image",
                    pipeline_scope="shared",
                    stage="C",
                    name="知识图谱字段提取",
                    version="component-v2",
                    system_prompt="提取知识图谱字段",
                    user_prompt="返回结构化结果",
                    rubric_version="rubric-v1",
                    status="published",
                    source="workflow_component",
                    created_by="ops",
                ),
            ]
        )
        add_active_v3_contract(db)
        db.commit()

        app.dependency_overrides[get_db] = lambda: (yield db)
        app.dependency_overrides[current_user] = lambda: user
        client = TestClient(app)

        missing = client.post(f"/api/baseline-sets/{baseline.id}/runs", json={})
        assert missing.status_code == 422
        assert missing.json()["detail"]["code"] == "stock_mechanism_required"

        overridden = client.post(
            f"/api/baseline-sets/{baseline.id}/runs",
            json={
                "workflow_version_id": version.id,
                "prompt_a_id": 9,
                "prompt_b_id": 10,
            },
        )
        assert overridden.status_code == 422
        assert overridden.json()["detail"]["code"] == (
            "stock_component_override_forbidden"
        )

        created = client.post(
            f"/api/baseline-sets/{baseline.id}/runs",
            json={"workflow_version_id": version.id},
        )
        assert created.status_code == 200, created.text
        assert created.json()["job_ids"] == []
        assert created.json()["workflow_run_count"] == 1
        run = db.get(BaselineRegressionRun, created.json()["id"])
        assert run is not None
        assert run.execution_engine == "native_workflow"
        assert run.workflow_version_id == version.id
        assert run.strategy_bundle_id is None
        assert db.scalar(select(func.count(EvaluationJob.id))) == 0
        assert db.scalar(select(func.count(ProductionRun.id))) == 1
    finally:
        app.dependency_overrides.clear()
        db.close()
        engine.dispose()


def test_baseline_selection_exposes_every_frozen_component_and_model_parameter() -> None:
    manifest = {
        "schema_version": "workflow-v3",
        "lane_key": "stock_regression",
        "nodes": [
            {
                "node_instance_id": "prompt_c",
                "kind": "prompt",
                "display_name": "知识图谱字段提取",
                "config": {
                    "asset_key": "prompt_c",
                    "max_tokens": 8192,
                    "thinking_level": "high",
                    "component_snapshot": {
                        "version_id": 31,
                        "version": "component-v2",
                        "content_hash": "a" * 64,
                    },
                    "model_snapshot": {
                        "model_registry_id": 7,
                        "name": "主评测模型",
                    },
                },
            },
            {
                "node_instance_id": "redline_1",
                "kind": "rule_module",
                "display_name": "准入与红线",
                "config": {
                    "asset_key": "eligibility_redline",
                    "module_type_key": "eligibility_redline",
                    "component_snapshot": {
                        "version_id": 41,
                        "version": "3",
                        "content_hash": "b" * 64,
                    },
                },
            },
        ],
    }
    run = SimpleNamespace(
        strategy_snapshot_json="{}",
        execution_snapshot_json=canonical_json(
            {
                "category_key": "space_image",
                "workflow_snapshot": {
                    "workflow": {
                        "workflow_definition_id": 1,
                        "workflow_version_id": 2,
                        "version": "3",
                        "canonical_hash": "c" * 64,
                        "manifest": manifest,
                    }
                },
            }
        ),
    )

    selection = _baseline_run_selection(run)

    components = selection["workflow"]["components"]
    assert [item["node_instance_id"] for item in components] == [
        "prompt_c",
        "redline_1",
    ]
    assert components[0]["component_version"]["version"] == "component-v2"
    assert components[0]["model"]["name"] == "主评测模型"
    assert components[0]["selected_parameters"] == {
        "max_tokens": 8192,
        "thinking_level": "none",
        "thinking_enabled": False,
    }
    assert components[1]["module_type_key"] == "eligibility_redline"

        "contract_key": contract.contract_key,
        "contract_hash": contract.snapshot()["contract_hash"],
        "route": {
            "site_scope": "domestic",
            "asset_scope": "single",
            "locale": "zh",
        },
    }

===== FILE backend/tests/test_storage_archive.py =====
"""Storage safety: verified binary logs and explicit keep-two full backup retention."""
import hashlib
import importlib.util
import json
from pathlib import Path

import pytest

SOURCE = Path(__file__).parents[2] / 'deploy/server/storage_archive.py'
spec = importlib.util.spec_from_file_location('storage_archive', SOURCE)
archive = importlib.util.module_from_spec(spec)
spec.loader.exec_module(archive)


def test_legacy_keep_two_cli_cannot_bypass_the_new_daily_policy(monkeypatch):
    import sys
    monkeypatch.setattr(sys, 'argv', ['storage_archive.py', 'retention', '--commit'])
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match='legacy keep-two retention disabled'):
        archive.main()


def backup(root, name='predeploy-fixture-20260901T000000Z'):
    folder = root / name
    folder.mkdir(parents=True)
    (folder / 'mysql.sql').write_bytes(b'CREATE TABLE fixture(id INT PRIMARY KEY);\n' * 10000)
    (folder / 'platform-data.tar.gz').write_bytes(b'opaque-original-archive\x00\xff')
    files = [{'path': n, 'size': (folder / n).stat().st_size,
              'sha256': hashlib.sha256((folder / n).read_bytes()).hexdigest()}
             for n in ('mysql.sql', 'platform-data.tar.gz')]
    (folder / 'manifest.json').write_text(json.dumps({
        'format': 'label-system-server-backup-v1', 'source_schema': 'fixture', 'files': files}))
    return folder


def test_binary_log_archive_roundtrip_preserves_exact_bytes(tmp_path):
    source = tmp_path / 'binlog.000001'
    source.write_bytes(b'\xfebin' + bytes(range(256)) * 10000)
    receipt = archive.archive_binary(source, tmp_path / 'cold')
    assert source.exists()  # Only MySQL PURGE owns live log deletion.
    assert receipt['sha256'] == hashlib.sha256(source.read_bytes()).hexdigest()
    assert archive.verify_binary(Path(receipt['archive']), receipt) is True


def test_binary_log_archive_resumes_after_missing_receipt(tmp_path):
    source = tmp_path / 'binlog.000001'
    source.write_bytes(b'\xfebin' + bytes(range(256)) * 10000)
    receipt = archive.archive_binary(source, tmp_path / 'cold')
    Path(receipt['archive'] + '.json').unlink()
    retried = archive.archive_binary(source, tmp_path / 'cold')
    assert retried['sha256'] == hashlib.sha256(source.read_bytes()).hexdigest()
    assert source.exists()


def test_sql_log_purge_refuses_unverified_chain():
    calls = []
    logs = [('binlog.000101', 100), ('binlog.000102', 200), ('binlog.000103', 300)]
    with pytest.raises(ValueError):
        archive.purge_verified_prefix(logs, {'binlog.000101': {'verified': False}}, calls.append)
    assert calls == []


def test_sql_log_purge_stops_before_active_and_unarchived_log():
    calls = []
    logs = [('binlog.000101', 100), ('binlog.000102', 200), ('binlog.000103', 300)]
    archive.purge_verified_prefix(logs, {'binlog.000101': {'verified': True, 'bytes': 100}}, calls.append)
    assert calls == ["PURGE BINARY LOGS TO 'binlog.000102'"]


def test_retention_removes_older_complete_backups_keeps_latest_two_bytes(tmp_path):
    root = tmp_path / 'backups'
    for day in range(1, 6):
        backup(root, f'predeploy-fixture-2026090{day}T000000Z')
    newest = root / 'predeploy-fixture-20260905T000000Z'
    expected = {p.name: p.read_bytes() for p in newest.iterdir()}
    plan = archive.retain_backups(root, expected_schema='fixture', keep=2, commit=False)
    assert len(plan['delete']) == 3 and len(plan['keep']) == 2
    assert len(list(root.iterdir())) == 5  # Preview has no filesystem mutation.
    result = archive.retain_backups(root, expected_schema='fixture', keep=2, commit=True)
    assert result['deleted_count'] == 3
    assert {p.name for p in root.iterdir() if p.name.startswith('predeploy')} == {
        'predeploy-fixture-20260904T000000Z', 'predeploy-fixture-20260905T000000Z'}
    assert {p.name: p.read_bytes() for p in newest.iterdir()} == expected
    assert archive.retain_backups(root, expected_schema='fixture', keep=2, commit=True)['deleted_count'] == 0


def test_corrupt_newest_backup_blocks_all_old_backup_deletion(tmp_path):
    root = tmp_path / 'backups'
    for day in range(1, 4):
        backup(root, f'predeploy-fixture-2026090{day}T000000Z')
    newest = root / 'predeploy-fixture-20260903T000000Z' / 'mysql.sql'
    before = newest.read_bytes()
    newest.write_bytes(b'X' + before[1:])  # Same size; must verify actual SHA.
    with pytest.raises(ValueError):
        archive.retain_backups(root, expected_schema='fixture', keep=2, commit=True)
    assert len(list(root.iterdir())) == 3


def test_unfinished_backup_and_other_directories_are_never_age_deleted(tmp_path):
    root = tmp_path / 'backups'
    for day in range(1, 4):
        backup(root, f'predeploy-fixture-2026090{day}T000000Z')
    incomplete = root / 'predeploy-fixture-20260904T000000Z'
    incomplete.mkdir()
    (incomplete / 'mysql.raw.sql').write_text('unfinished')
    unrelated = root / 'binlog-space-recovery-20260911'
    unrelated.mkdir()
    (unrelated / 'binlog.000001').write_text('evidence')
    result = archive.retain_backups(root, expected_schema='fixture', keep=2, commit=True)
    assert result['deleted_count'] == 1
    assert (incomplete / 'mysql.raw.sql').read_text() == 'unfinished'
    assert (unrelated / 'binlog.000001').read_text() == 'evidence'


def test_symlink_backup_file_blocks_retention_and_preserves_other_file(tmp_path):
    root = tmp_path / 'backups'
    for day in range(1, 4):
        backup(root, f'predeploy-fixture-2026090{day}T000000Z')
    target = tmp_path / 'unrelated'
    target.write_text('safe')
    link = root / 'predeploy-fixture-20260901T000000Z' / 'mysql.sql'
    link.unlink()
    link.symlink_to(target)
    with pytest.raises(ValueError):
        archive.retain_backups(root, expected_schema='fixture', keep=2, commit=True)
    assert target.read_text() == 'safe'


def test_two_newer_backups_from_wrong_schema_cannot_replace_correct_environment(tmp_path):
    root = tmp_path / 'backups'
    for day in range(1, 4):
        folder = backup(root, f'predeploy-fixture-2026090{day}T000000Z')
        if day > 1:
            p = folder / 'manifest.json'
            m = json.loads(p.read_text())
            m['source_schema'] = 'different_environment'
            p.write_text(json.dumps(m))
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match='schema'):
        archive.retain_backups(root, expected_schema='fixture', commit=True)
    assert len(list(root.iterdir())) == 3


def test_truncated_receipt_is_rebuilt_only_after_source_and_archive_match(tmp_path):
    source = tmp_path / 'binlog.000001'
    source.write_bytes(b'\xfebin' + bytes(range(256)) * 10000)
    r = archive.archive_binary(source, tmp_path / 'cold')
    receipt = Path(r['archive'] + '.json')
    receipt.write_text('{"incomplete":')
    rebuilt = archive.archive_binary(source, tmp_path / 'cold')
    assert rebuilt['sha256'] == hashlib.sha256(source.read_bytes()).hexdigest()
    assert len(list(receipt.parent.glob(receipt.name + '.invalid-*'))) == 1
    assert archive.archive_binary(source, tmp_path / 'cold') == rebuilt
    assert source.exists()


def test_failed_receipt_serialization_leaves_no_partial_final_path(tmp_path):
    receipt = tmp_path / 'receipt.json'
    with pytest.raises(TypeError):
        archive.write_json(receipt, {'cannot_encode': object()})
    assert not receipt.exists()
    archive.write_json(receipt, {'verified': True})
    assert json.loads(receipt.read_text()) == {'verified': True}


def test_binlog_archive_follows_the_live_datadir_within_the_allowlist(tmp_path, monkeypatch):
    """.85 的 datadir 是 /var/lib/mysql/，.137 是 /var/lib/mysql/data/。09-29 钉死 .137 布局的版本装到 .85 后，
    凡有 binlog 可归档的轮次都报 replication dependency or database identity changed。现在按实际 datadir 归档，
    其它 datadir 仍视为身份变化拒绝。"""
    sa = archive
    answers = {'SELECT COUNT(*) FROM performance_schema.replication_connection_configuration': '0',
               "SELECT COUNT(*) FROM mysql.user WHERE Repl_slave_priv='Y' AND NOT (User='root' AND Host='localhost' AND plugin='auth_socket')": '0',
               'SELECT COUNT(*) FROM mysql.role_edges': '0'}
    for datadir in ('/var/lib/mysql/', '/var/lib/mysql/data/'):
        monkeypatch.setattr(sa, 'mysql', lambda q, d=datadir: d if q == 'SELECT @@datadir' else answers[q])
        sa.no_replication()
        assert sa.live_datadir() == Path(datadir)
    monkeypatch.setattr(sa, 'mysql', lambda q: '/srv/mysql/' if q == 'SELECT @@datadir' else answers[q])
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match='identity changed'):
        sa.no_replication()
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match='identity changed'):
        sa.live_datadir()


def test_volume_report_skips_missing_mounts(monkeypatch, tmp_path):
    sa = archive
    monkeypatch.setattr(sa, 'VOLUMES', (str(tmp_path), str(tmp_path / 'absent')))
    report = sa.report()
    assert [v['path'] for v in report['volumes']] == [str(tmp_path)]

===== FILE backend/tests/test_strategy_bundle.py =====
"""Acceptance tests for immutable StrategyBundle history."""
from __future__ import annotations

import json
from concurrent.futures import ThreadPoolExecutor
from datetime import datetime, timezone

from app import models  # noqa: F401

from app.database import Base
from app.migrations import run_migrations

    Asset,
    EvaluationJob,
    EvaluationResult,
    ModelConfig,

    SamplingPolicy,

    StrategyBundleImmutableError,
    StrategySnapshotRequiredError,
)
from app.strategy_bundle import (

@pytest.fixture
def db(tmp_path) -> Session:
    engine = create_engine(
        f"sqlite:///{tmp_path / 'strategy.db'}",
        connect_args={"check_same_thread": False},
    )
    Base.metadata.create_all(bind=engine)
    with engine.begin() as connection:
        run_migrations(connection)
    session = Session(engine, expire_on_commit=False)
    try:
        yield session
    finally:
        session.close()
        engine.dispose()


def _seed_strategy_inputs(
    db: Session,
    *,
    base_url: str = "https://example.test/v1",
    api_path: str = "/chat/completions",
    prompt_a_system: str = "System A",
    prompt_a_user: str = "User A",
) -> tuple[ModelConfig, PromptVersion, PromptVersion, SamplingPolicy]:
    model_config = ModelConfig(
        name="Test Model",
        provider="doubao",
        base_url=base_url,
        api_path=api_path,
        model_id="test-model-v1",
        encrypted_api_key="encrypted-key-never-snapshot",
        temperature=0.1,
        max_tokens=4096,
        timeout_seconds=120,
        max_retries=1,
        max_concurrency=2,
        structured_output=True,
        high_risk_review_enabled=True,
    )
    prompt_a = PromptVersion(
        stage="A",
        name="Test A",
        version="A-v1",
        system_prompt=prompt_a_system,
        user_prompt=prompt_a_user,
        rubric_version="rubric-v1",
        status="published",
    )
    prompt_b = PromptVersion(
        stage="B",
        name="Test B",
        version="B-v1",
        system_prompt="System B",
        user_prompt="User B",
        rubric_version="rubric-v1",
        status="published",
    )
    policy = SamplingPolicy(
        id=1,
        revision=3,
        sample_rate=10,
        low_confidence_threshold=0.7,
        medium_confidence_threshold=0.9,
        cold_start_required_count=5,
        high_level_required_from=4,
    )
    db.add_all([model_config, prompt_a, prompt_b, policy])
    db.commit()
    return model_config, prompt_a, prompt_b, policy


def _bundle(
    db: Session,
    model_config: ModelConfig,
    prompt_a: PromptVersion,
    prompt_b: PromptVersion | None,
    policy: SamplingPolicy | None,
    *,
    rubric_version: str = "rubric-v1",
    engine_version: str = "engine-v2.5.0",
    risk_review_version: str | None = "risk-v1",
) -> StrategyBundle:
    bundle = get_or_create_bundle(
        db=db,
        model_config=model_config,
        prompt_a=prompt_a,
        prompt_b=prompt_b,
        rubric_version=rubric_version,
        engine_version=engine_version,
        risk_review_version=risk_review_version,
        sampling_policy=policy,
    )
    db.commit()
    return bundle


def _asset_and_job(db: Session) -> tuple[Asset, EvaluationJob]:
    asset = Asset(
        original_name="test.jpg",
        stored_name="stored.jpg",
        mime_type="image/jpeg",
        size_bytes=1000,
        sha256="a" * 64,
    )
    db.add(asset)
    db.flush()
    job = EvaluationJob(asset_id=asset.id, status="completed")
    db.add(job)
    db.commit()
    return asset, job


def _evaluation(
    *,
    asset: Asset,
    job: EvaluationJob,
    bundle_id: int | None,
    snapshot_json: str | None,
) -> EvaluationResult:
    return EvaluationResult(
        asset_id=asset.id,
        job_id=job.id,
        strategy_bundle_id=bundle_id,
        strategy_snapshot_json=snapshot_json,
        precheck_json="{}",
        scoring_json="{}",
        raw_response_a="{}",
        model_id="test-model-v1",
        prompt_a_version="A-v1",
        prompt_b_version="B-v1",
        risk_review_version="risk-v1",
        rubric_version="rubric-v1",
        engine_version="engine-v2.5.0",
    )


def test_identical_canonical_definition_reuses_one_bundle(db: Session) -> None:
    model_config, prompt_a, prompt_b, policy = _seed_strategy_inputs(db)

    first = _bundle(db, model_config, prompt_a, prompt_b, policy)
    second = _bundle(db, model_config, prompt_a, prompt_b, policy)

    assert second.id == first.id
    assert second.canonical_hash == first.canonical_hash
    assert db.scalar(select(func.count()).select_from(StrategyBundle)) == 1


def test_concurrent_sqlite_workers_reuse_the_same_bundle(
    monkeypatch,
    tmp_path,
) -> None:
    engine = create_engine(
        f"sqlite:///{tmp_path / 'bundle-race.db'}",
        connect_args={"check_same_thread": False, "timeout": 10},
    )
    Base.metadata.create_all(bind=engine)
    with engine.begin() as connection:
        run_migrations(connection)
    factory = sessionmaker(bind=engine, expire_on_commit=False)
    with factory() as db:
        model_config, prompt_a, prompt_b, policy = (
            _seed_strategy_inputs(db)
        )
        input_ids = (
            model_config.id,
            prompt_a.id,
            prompt_b.id,
            policy.id,
        )

    barrier = Barrier(2)
    original_insert = strategy_bundle_module._insert_bundle_if_absent

    def synchronized_insert(session, values):
        barrier.wait(timeout=5)
        return original_insert(session, values)

    monkeypatch.setattr(
        strategy_bundle_module,
        "_insert_bundle_if_absent",
        synchronized_insert,
    )

    def create_from_worker() -> int:
        with factory() as db:
            model_config = db.get(ModelConfig, input_ids[0])
            prompt_a = db.get(PromptVersion, input_ids[1])
            prompt_b = db.get(PromptVersion, input_ids[2])
            policy = db.get(SamplingPolicy, input_ids[3])
            assert model_config and prompt_a and prompt_b and policy
            bundle = get_or_create_bundle(
                db,
                model_config,
                prompt_a,
                prompt_b,
                "rubric-v1",
                "engine-v2.5.0",
                "risk-v1",
                policy,
            )
            db.commit()
            return bundle.id

    try:
        with ThreadPoolExecutor(max_workers=2) as pool:
            ids = list(pool.map(lambda _: create_from_worker(), range(2)))
        assert ids[0] == ids[1]
        with factory() as db:
            assert (
                db.scalar(
                    select(func.count()).select_from(StrategyBundle)
                )
                == 1
            )
    finally:
        engine.dispose()


def test_noncanonical_integrity_error_is_not_swallowed(
    db: Session,
    monkeypatch,
) -> None:
    model_config, prompt_a, prompt_b, policy = _seed_strategy_inputs(db)
    original_values = strategy_bundle_module._bundle_values

    def invalid_values(**kwargs):
        values = original_values(**kwargs)
        values["model_id"] = None
        return values

    monkeypatch.setattr(
        strategy_bundle_module,
        "_bundle_values",
        invalid_values,
    )
    with pytest.raises(IntegrityError, match="model_id"):
        get_or_create_bundle(
            db,
            model_config,
            prompt_a,
            prompt_b,
            "rubric-v1",
            "engine-v2.5.0",
            "risk-v1",
            policy,
        )
    db.rollback()
    assert db.scalar(select(func.count()).select_from(StrategyBundle)) == 0


@pytest.mark.parametrize(
    "leak_value",
    [
        "client_secret=late-bundle-value-sentinel",
        "API-KEY/late-bundle-value-sentinel",
    ],
)
def test_final_recursive_gate_rejects_late_bundle_value_leak(
    db: Session,
    monkeypatch,
    leak_value: str,
) -> None:
    sentinel = "late-bundle-value-sentinel"
    model_config, prompt_a, prompt_b, policy = _seed_strategy_inputs(db)
    original_values = strategy_bundle_module._bundle_values

    def unsafe_values(**kwargs):
        values = original_values(**kwargs)
        values["model_config_snapshot"] = json.dumps(
            {
                "base_url": "https://example.test/v1",
                "note": leak_value,
            }
        )
        return values

    monkeypatch.setattr(
        strategy_bundle_module,
        "_bundle_values",
        unsafe_values,
    )
    with pytest.raises(StrategySecretError) as caught:
        get_or_create_bundle(
            db,
            model_config,
            prompt_a,
            prompt_b,
            "rubric-v1",
            "engine-v2.5.0",
            "risk-v1",
            policy,
        )

    assert sentinel not in str(caught.value)
    bundle_database_text = json.dumps(
        [
            {
                key: value
                for key, value in vars(bundle).items()
                if not key.startswith("_")
            }
            for bundle in db.scalars(select(StrategyBundle)).all()
        ],
        ensure_ascii=False,
        default=str,
    )
    assert sentinel not in bundle_database_text
    assert (
        db.scalar(select(func.count()).select_from(StrategyBundle))
        == 0
    )


def test_prompt_persistent_id_and_exact_body_are_part_of_hash(db: Session) -> None:
    model_config, prompt_a, prompt_b, policy = _seed_strategy_inputs(db)
    original = _bundle(db, model_config, prompt_a, prompt_b, policy)

    fields = dict(
        category_key=prompt_a.category_key,
        pipeline_scope=prompt_a.pipeline_scope,
        stage=prompt_a.stage,
        name=prompt_a.name,
        version=prompt_a.version,
        system_prompt=prompt_a.system_prompt,
        user_prompt=prompt_a.user_prompt,
        rubric_version=prompt_a.rubric_version,
    )
    # 迁移 129 起 (category_key, stage, version) 唯一：同版本号的第二行插不进去了。
    db.add(PromptVersion(**fields, status="published"))
    with pytest.raises(IntegrityError):
        db.commit()
    db.rollback()
    # 历史库里仍有同内容不同 id 的行（129 改名前）：持久 id 必须是哈希身份的一部分。
    original_definition = strategy_bundle_module._build_prompt_definition(prompt_a)
    twin_definition = strategy_bundle_module._build_prompt_definition(
        PromptVersion(id=prompt_a.id + 1000, **fields)
    )
    assert {
        key for key in original_definition
        if original_definition[key] != twin_definition[key]
    } == {"id"}

    prompt_a.system_prompt = "System A changed without changing version"
    changed_body = _bundle(db, model_config, prompt_a, prompt_b, policy)
    assert changed_body.canonical_hash != original.canonical_hash
    assert changed_body.id != original.id

    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="当前策略定义"):
        build_strategy_snapshot(original, prompt_a, prompt_b, policy)


def test_hash_covers_model_rubric_engine_risk_and_sampling(db: Session) -> None:
    model_config, prompt_a, prompt_b, policy = _seed_strategy_inputs(db)
    hashes = {
        _bundle(db, model_config, prompt_a, prompt_b, policy).canonical_hash
    }

    model_config.temperature = 0.2
    hashes.add(_bundle(db, model_config, prompt_a, prompt_b, policy).canonical_hash)

    policy.sample_rate = 25
    hashes.add(_bundle(db, model_config, prompt_a, prompt_b, policy).canonical_hash)

    hashes.add(
        _bundle(
            db,
            model_config,
            prompt_a,
            prompt_b,
            policy,
            rubric_version="rubric-v2",
        ).canonical_hash
    )
    hashes.add(
        _bundle(
            db,
            model_config,
            prompt_a,
            prompt_b,
            policy,
            engine_version="engine-v3",
        ).canonical_hash
    )
    hashes.add(
        _bundle(
            db,
            model_config,
            prompt_a,
            prompt_b,
            policy,
            risk_review_version="risk-v2",
        ).canonical_hash
    )

    assert len(hashes) == 6


def test_secret_rotation_does_not_change_hash(db: Session) -> None:

    first = _bundle(db, model_config, prompt_a, prompt_b, policy)

    model_config.encrypted_api_key = "different-encrypted-secret"
    second = _bundle(db, model_config, prompt_a, prompt_b, policy)

def test_strategy_creation_rejects_endpoint_and_header_credentials(
    db: Session,
) -> None:
    secrets = {
        "user": "endpoint-user",
        "password": "endpoint-password",
        "query": "query-api-secret",
        "auth_query": "query-auth-secret",
        "path": "path-credential-secret",
        "path_query": "path-token-secret",
        "prompt_auth": "prompt-bearer-secret",
        "prompt_key": "prompt-api-secret",
        "nested": "nested-cookie-secret",
    }
    model_config, prompt_a, prompt_b, policy = _seed_strategy_inputs(
        db,
        base_url=(
            f"https://{secrets['user']}:{secrets['password']}@Example.Test:443"
            f"/v1/token/{secrets['path']}?region=cn&api_key={secrets['query']}"
            f"&auth={secrets['auth_query']}"
        ),
        api_path=f"/chat/completions?token={secrets['path_query']}&format=json",
        prompt_a_system=f"Authorization: Bearer {secrets['prompt_auth']}",
        prompt_a_user=f"x-api-key: {secrets['prompt_key']}",
    )
    with pytest.raises(StrategySecretError) as caught:
        _bundle(db, model_config, prompt_a, prompt_b, policy)
    for secret in secrets.values():
        assert secret not in str(caught.value)
    assert db.scalar(select(func.count()).select_from(StrategyBundle)) == 0
    sanitized = _redact_secrets(
        {
            "base_url": model_config.base_url,
            "api_path": model_config.api_path,
            "system_prompt": prompt_a.system_prompt,
            "user_prompt": prompt_a.user_prompt,
        }
    )
    base_url = urlsplit(sanitized["base_url"])
    assert base_url.netloc == "example.test"
    assert base_url.path == f"/v1/token/{REDACTED}"
    assert parse_qs(base_url.query) == {
        "api_key": [REDACTED],
        "auth": [REDACTED],
        "region": ["cn"],
    }
    api_path = urlsplit(sanitized["api_path"])
    assert api_path.path == "/chat/completions"
    assert parse_qs(api_path.query) == {
        "format": ["json"],
        "token": [REDACTED],
    }
    assert sanitized["system_prompt"] == f"Authorization: {REDACTED}"
    assert sanitized["user_prompt"] == f"x-api-key: {REDACTED}"

    nested = _redact_secrets(
        {
            "transport": {
                "headers": {
                    "Authorization": "Basic dXNlcjpwYXNz",
                    "Cookie": f"session={secrets['nested']}",
                    "Accept": "application/json",
                }
            }
        }
    )
    assert nested["transport"]["headers"] == {
        "Authorization": REDACTED,
        "Cookie": REDACTED,
        "Accept": "application/json",
    }


def test_model_snapshot_records_thinking_mode_without_secrets(db: Session) -> None:
    model_config, _prompt_a, _prompt_b, _policy = _seed_strategy_inputs(db)
    model_config.thinking_mode = "disabled"
    db.commit()
    snapshot = strategy_bundle_module.build_model_config_snapshot(model_config)
    assert snapshot["thinking_mode"] == "disabled"
    assert "encrypted_api_key" not in snapshot


def test_serialized_prompt_credentials_fail_closed(
    db: Session,
) -> None:
    api_secret = "serialized-api-key-secret"
    auth_secret = "serialized-authorization-secret"
    model_config, prompt_a, prompt_b, policy = _seed_strategy_inputs(
        db,
        prompt_a_system=json.dumps(
            {
                "api_key": api_secret,
                "authorization_header": (
                    f"Bearer {auth_secret}"
                ),
                "business_key": "catalog-entry",
                "keyword": "tokenization quality",
            }
        ),
    )
    with pytest.raises(StrategySecretError) as serialized_error:
        _bundle(db, model_config, prompt_a, prompt_b, policy)
    assert api_secret not in str(serialized_error.value)
    assert auth_secret not in str(serialized_error.value)
    assert db.scalar(select(func.count()).select_from(StrategyBundle)) == 0

    prompt_a.system_prompt = (
        '{"api_key":"malformed-secret-must-not-echo"'
    )
    before = db.scalar(
        select(func.count()).select_from(StrategyBundle)
    )
    with pytest.raises(StrategySecretError) as caught:
        get_or_create_bundle(
            db,
            model_config,
            prompt_a,
            prompt_b,
            "rubric-v1",
            "engine-v2.5.0",
            "risk-v1",
            policy,
        )
    assert "malformed-secret-must-not-echo" not in str(caught.value)
    assert (
        db.scalar(select(func.count()).select_from(StrategyBundle))
        == before
    )


def test_business_auth_language_and_safe_json_prompt_remain_exact(
    db: Session,
) -> None:
    business_description = (
        "该供应商使用 Basic authentication 模式，无任何真实凭据；"
        "Bearer token 机制也仅为业务描述。"
        "Bearer token mechanism and Bearer authentication mode "
        "are documentation terms."
    )
    safe_json_prompt = (
        '{ "business_key": "business_key/foo", '
        '"keyword": "keyword/path" }'
    )
    model_config, prompt_a, prompt_b, policy = _seed_strategy_inputs(
        db,
        prompt_a_system=business_description,
        prompt_a_user=safe_json_prompt,
    )

    bundle = _bundle(db, model_config, prompt_a, prompt_b, policy)
    snapshot = json.loads(
        build_strategy_snapshot(bundle, prompt_a, prompt_b, policy)
    )

    assert snapshot["prompt_a"]["system_prompt"] == business_description
    assert snapshot["prompt_a"]["user_prompt"] == safe_json_prompt
    assert json.loads(bundle.model_config_snapshot)["base_url"] == (
        "https://example.test/v1"
    )


@pytest.mark.parametrize(
    "credential_text",
    [
        "Authorization: Basic dXNlcjpwYXNz",
        (
            "Authorization: Bearer "
            "eyJhbGciOiJIUzI1NiJ9.eyJzdWIiOiIxIn0.signature123"
        ),
        "Bearer sk-example-secret",
        "Bearer abcdefghijklmnopq",
        "Basic dXNlcjpwYXNz",
    ],
)
def test_credential_shaped_basic_and_bearer_prompts_are_rejected(
    db: Session,
    credential_text: str,
) -> None:
    model_config, prompt_a, prompt_b, policy = _seed_strategy_inputs(
        db,
        prompt_a_system=credential_text,
    )

    with pytest.raises(StrategySecretError) as caught:
        _bundle(db, model_config, prompt_a, prompt_b, policy)

    assert credential_text not in str(caught.value)
    assert db.scalar(select(func.count()).select_from(StrategyBundle)) == 0


def test_slash_secret_aliases_fail_closed_across_strategy_sources_and_bytes(
    tmp_path,
) -> None:
    database_path = tmp_path / "strategy-slash.db"
    engine = create_engine(f"sqlite:///{database_path}")
    Base.metadata.create_all(bind=engine)
    session = Session(engine, expire_on_commit=False)
    sentinels: list[str] = []
    try:
        model_config, prompt_a, prompt_b, policy = _seed_strategy_inputs(
            session
        )
        aliases = (
            "AUTHORIZATION_HEADER",
            "Authorization-Header",
            "CLIENT_SECRET",
            "Client-Secret",
            "X_AUTH_TOKEN",
            "X-Auth-Token",
            "API_KEY",
            "Api-Key",
            "COOKIE",
        )
        locations = (
            "config",
            "model",
            "prompt",
            "nested_prompt",
            "url_path",
            "url_query",
        )

        for alias_index, alias in enumerate(aliases):
            for location_index, location in enumerate(locations):
                sentinel = (
                    f"strategy-slash-{alias_index}-{location_index}-sentinel"
                )
                sentinels.append(sentinel)
                malicious_value = f"{alias}/{sentinel}"
                if location == "config":
                    model_config.name = malicious_value
                elif location == "model":
                    model_config.model_id = malicious_value
                elif location == "prompt":
                    prompt_a.system_prompt = malicious_value
                elif location == "nested_prompt":
                    prompt_a.system_prompt = json.dumps(
                        {"outer": {"note": malicious_value}}
                    )
                elif location == "url_path":
                    model_config.base_url = (
                        f"https://example.test/v1/{malicious_value}"
                    )
                else:
                    model_config.base_url = (
                        "https://example.test/v1"
                        f"?next={malicious_value}"
                    )

                with pytest.raises(StrategySecretError) as caught:
                    get_or_create_bundle(
                        session,
                        model_config,
                        prompt_a,
                        prompt_b,
                        "rubric-v1",
                        "engine-v2.5.0",
                        "risk-v1",
                        policy,
                    )
                assert alias not in str(caught.value)
                assert sentinel not in str(caught.value)
                session.rollback()

        assert (
            session.scalar(select(func.count()).select_from(StrategyBundle))
            == 0
        )
    finally:
        session.close()
        engine.dispose()

    raw_database = database_path.read_bytes()
    for sentinel in sentinels:
        assert sentinel.encode() not in raw_database


@pytest.mark.parametrize(
    "base_url_template",
    [
        "https://example.test:bad/v1?client_secret={sentinel}",
        "https://example.test:bad/v1?authorization_header={sentinel}",
        "https://example.test:bad/v1?API-key={sentinel}",
        "https://user:{sentinel}@example.test:bad/v1",
    ],
)
def test_malformed_endpoint_credentials_fail_closed_before_bundle_write(
    db: Session,
    base_url_template: str,
) -> None:
    sentinel = "malformed-endpoint-sentinel"
    model_config, prompt_a, prompt_b, policy = _seed_strategy_inputs(
        db,
        base_url=base_url_template.format(sentinel=sentinel),
    )
    before = db.scalar(select(func.count()).select_from(StrategyBundle))

    with pytest.raises(StrategySecretError) as caught:
        get_or_create_bundle(
            db,
            model_config,
            prompt_a,
            prompt_b,
            "rubric-v1",
            "engine-v2.5.0",
            "risk-v1",
            policy,
        )

    assert sentinel not in str(caught.value)
    bundles = db.scalars(select(StrategyBundle)).all()
    bundle_database_text = json.dumps(
        [
            {
                key: value
                for key, value in vars(bundle).items()
                if not key.startswith("_")
            }
            for bundle in bundles
        ],
        ensure_ascii=False,
        default=str,
    )
    assert sentinel not in bundle_database_text
    assert len(bundles) == before


def test_nested_serialized_endpoint_and_prompt_credentials_fail_closed(
    db: Session,
) -> None:
    endpoint_sentinel = "nested-endpoint-sentinel"
    model_config, prompt_a, prompt_b, policy = _seed_strategy_inputs(
        db,
        base_url=json.dumps(
            {
                "transport": {
                    "url": (
                        "https://example.test/v1"
                        f"?client_secret={endpoint_sentinel}"
                    )
                }
            }
        ),
    )
    with pytest.raises(StrategySecretError) as endpoint_error:
        get_or_create_bundle(
            db,
            model_config,
            prompt_a,
            prompt_b,
            "rubric-v1",
            "engine-v2.5.0",
            "risk-v1",
            policy,
        )
    assert endpoint_sentinel not in str(endpoint_error.value)

    model_config.base_url = "https://example.test/v1"
    prompt_sentinel = "nested-prompt-sentinel"
    prompt_a.system_prompt = json.dumps(
        {
            "outer": {
                "note": f"client-secret:{prompt_sentinel}",
                "business_key": "catalog-entry",
                "keyword": "tokenization quality",
            }
        }
    )
    with pytest.raises(StrategySecretError) as prompt_error:
        get_or_create_bundle(
            db,
            model_config,
            prompt_a,
            prompt_b,
            "rubric-v1",
            "engine-v2.5.0",
            "risk-v1",
            policy,
        )
    assert prompt_sentinel not in str(prompt_error.value)
    assert (
        db.scalar(select(func.count()).select_from(StrategyBundle))
        == 0
    )


def test_credential_free_endpoint_is_persisted_unchanged(db: Session) -> None:
    base_url = "https://Example.Test:443/v1?region=cn&keyword=render"
    model_config, prompt_a, prompt_b, policy = _seed_strategy_inputs(
        db,
        base_url=base_url,
    )
    bundle = _bundle(db, model_config, prompt_a, prompt_b, policy)
    snapshot = json.loads(bundle.model_config_snapshot)

    assert snapshot["base_url"] == (
        "https://example.test/v1?keyword=render&region=cn"
    )


_IMMUTABLE_FIELD_CHANGES = [
    ("canonical_hash", "f" * 64),
    ("model_id", "changed-model"),
    ("model_config_snapshot", '{"changed":true}'),
    ("prompt_a_version", "changed-a"),
    ("prompt_b_version", "changed-b"),
    ("rubric_version", "changed-rubric"),
    ("engine_version", "changed-engine"),
    ("sampling_policy_revision", 999),
    ("risk_review_version", "changed-risk"),
    ("created_at", datetime(2030, 1, 1, tzinfo=timezone.utc)),
]


@pytest.mark.parametrize(("field", "new_value"), _IMMUTABLE_FIELD_CHANGES)
def test_persisted_bundle_rejects_every_field_mutation(
    db: Session, field: str, new_value: object
) -> None:
    model_config, prompt_a, prompt_b, policy = _seed_strategy_inputs(db)
    bundle = _bundle(db, model_config, prompt_a, prompt_b, policy)
    original_id = bundle.id

    setattr(bundle, field, new_value)
    with pytest.raises(StrategyBundleImmutableError, match="禁止原地更新"):
        db.commit()
    db.rollback()

    persisted = db.get(StrategyBundle, original_id)
    assert persisted is not None
    assert getattr(persisted, field) != new_value


def test_persisted_bundle_rejects_orm_delete(db: Session) -> None:
    model_config, prompt_a, prompt_b, policy = _seed_strategy_inputs(db)
    bundle = _bundle(db, model_config, prompt_a, prompt_b, policy)
    bundle_id = bundle.id

    db.delete(bundle)
    with pytest.raises(StrategyBundleImmutableError, match="禁止删除"):
        db.commit()
    db.rollback()

    assert db.get(StrategyBundle, bundle_id) is not None


def test_new_result_persists_complete_bundle_snapshot(db: Session) -> None:
    model_config, prompt_a, prompt_b, policy = _seed_strategy_inputs(db)
    bundle = _bundle(db, model_config, prompt_a, prompt_b, policy)
    snapshot_json = build_evaluation_strategy_snapshot(
        db=db,
        bundle=bundle,
        prompt_a=prompt_a,
        prompt_b=prompt_b,
        sampling_policy=policy,
        aesthetic={"scoring_profile": "space_aesthetic_v1.3"},
    )
    asset, job = _asset_and_job(db)

    result = _evaluation(
        asset=asset,
        job=job,
        bundle_id=bundle.id,
        snapshot_json=snapshot_json,
    )
    db.add(result)
    db.commit()

    snapshot = json.loads(result.strategy_snapshot_json or "")
    assert result.strategy_bundle_id == bundle.id
    assert snapshot["bundle_id"] == bundle.id
    assert snapshot["canonical_hash"] == bundle.canonical_hash
    assert snapshot["prompt_a"]["id"] == prompt_a.id
    assert snapshot["prompt_b"]["id"] == prompt_b.id
    assert snapshot["sampling_policy"]["revision"] == policy.revision


@pytest.mark.parametrize(
    "invalid_kind",
    [
        "missing_bundle",
        "missing_snapshot",
        "empty_snapshot",
        "incomplete_snapshot",
        "mismatch",
        "hash_mismatch",
    ],
)
def test_new_result_rejects_missing_or_incomplete_strategy(
    db: Session, invalid_kind: str
) -> None:
    model_config, prompt_a, prompt_b, policy = _seed_strategy_inputs(db)
    bundle = _bundle(db, model_config, prompt_a, prompt_b, policy)
    snapshot_json = build_evaluation_strategy_snapshot(
        db=db,
        bundle=bundle,
        prompt_a=prompt_a,
        prompt_b=prompt_b,
        sampling_policy=policy,
        aesthetic={"scoring_profile": "space_aesthetic_v1.3"},
    )
    asset, job = _asset_and_job(db)
    bundle_id: int | None = bundle.id

    if invalid_kind == "missing_bundle":
        bundle_id = None
    elif invalid_kind == "missing_snapshot":
        snapshot_json = None
    elif invalid_kind == "empty_snapshot":
        snapshot_json = " "
    elif invalid_kind == "incomplete_snapshot":
        snapshot_json = "{}"
    elif invalid_kind == "mismatch":
        snapshot = json.loads(snapshot_json)
        snapshot["bundle_id"] = bundle.id + 1
        snapshot_json = json.dumps(snapshot)
    else:
        snapshot = json.loads(snapshot_json)
        snapshot["canonical_hash"] = "f" * 64
        snapshot_json = json.dumps(snapshot)

    db.add(
        _evaluation(
            asset=asset,
            job=job,
            bundle_id=bundle_id,
            snapshot_json=snapshot_json,
        )
    )
    with pytest.raises(StrategySnapshotRequiredError):
        db.commit()
    db.rollback()


def test_database_trigger_rejects_unbound_raw_result_insert(db: Session) -> None:
    asset, job = _asset_and_job(db)

    with pytest.raises(IntegrityError, match="strategy binding is required"):
        db.connection().exec_driver_sql(
            """
            INSERT INTO evaluation_results (
                asset_id, job_id, precheck_json, scoring_json, raw_response_a,
                needs_review, model_id, prompt_a_version, rubric_version,
                engine_version, created_at, updated_at
            )
            VALUES (?, ?, '{}', '{}', '{}', 0, 'model', 'A1', 'R1', 'E1',
                    CURRENT_TIMESTAMP, CURRENT_TIMESTAMP)
            """,
            (asset.id, job.id),
        )
    db.rollback()


def test_database_trigger_rejects_incomplete_raw_snapshot(db: Session) -> None:
    model_config, prompt_a, prompt_b, policy = _seed_strategy_inputs(db)
    bundle = _bundle(db, model_config, prompt_a, prompt_b, policy)
    asset, job = _asset_and_job(db)

    with pytest.raises(IntegrityError, match="strategy binding is required"):
        db.connection().exec_driver_sql(
            """
            INSERT INTO evaluation_results (
                asset_id, job_id, strategy_bundle_id, strategy_snapshot_json,
                precheck_json, scoring_json, raw_response_a, needs_review,
                model_id, prompt_a_version, prompt_b_version,
                risk_review_version, rubric_version, engine_version,
                created_at, updated_at
            )
            VALUES (
                ?, ?, ?, ?, '{}', '{}', '{}', 0,
                'test-model-v1', 'A-v1', 'B-v1', 'risk-v1',
                'rubric-v1', 'engine-v2.5.0',
                CURRENT_TIMESTAMP, CURRENT_TIMESTAMP
            )
            """,
            (
                asset.id,
                job.id,
                bundle.id,
                json.dumps({"bundle_id": bundle.id, "placeholder": True}),
            ),
        )
    db.rollback()

===== FILE backend/tests/test_strict_prompt_contracts.py =====
from __future__ import annotations

from copy import deepcopy
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Any, Mapping

import pytest

from app.inspiration_aesthetic_foundation import (
    DIMENSION_KEYS,
    FOUNDATION_VERSION,
)
from app.inspiration_category_seed import build_inspiration_v3_contract
from app.knowledge_graph_contracts import (
    get_knowledge_graph_contract,
    resolve_knowledge_graph_fixture,
)
from app.model_3d_su_category_seed import build_model_3d_su_contract
from app.prompt_output_contract import (
    PromptOutputContractError,
    parse_output_contract,
    validate_output_payload,
)
from app.strict_prompt_contracts import (
    LATEST_INSPIRATION_CONTRACT_PROFILE,
    merged_strict_output_contract,
    prompt_slimming_audit,
    strict_output_contract,
)


ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[2]
BACKEND_PROMPTS = ROOT / "backend" / "prompts"
LATEST_FOUNDATION_VERSION = "inspiration-aesthetic-foundation-v3"


def _frozen_contract(category: str) -> dict[str, Any]:
    if category == "inspiration_image":
        return build_inspiration_v3_contract()
    if category == "model_3d_su":
        return build_model_3d_su_contract()
    raise AssertionError(category)


def _assert_every_object_is_closed_and_required(schema: Mapping[str, Any]) -> None:
    if schema.get("type") == "object":
        properties = schema["properties"]
        assert schema["additionalProperties"] is False
        assert schema["required"] == list(properties)
        for child in properties.values():
            _assert_every_object_is_closed_and_required(child)
    elif schema.get("type") == "array":
        _assert_every_object_is_closed_and_required(schema["items"])


@pytest.mark.parametrize(
    ("category", "stage"),
    [
        ("inspiration_image", "A"),
        ("inspiration_image", "B"),
        ("model_3d_su", "A"),
        ("model_3d_su", "B"),
    ],
)
def test_every_fixed_field_is_required_and_objects_are_closed(
    category: str,
    stage: str,
) -> None:
    contract = strict_output_contract(
        category,
        stage,
        evaluation_contract=_frozen_contract(category),
    )
    parsed = parse_output_contract(contract)
    assert parsed.mode == "json_schema"
    assert parsed.strict is True
    _assert_every_object_is_closed_and_required(parsed.schema)


def test_latest_inspiration_single_contract_is_the_closed_union_of_a_and_b() -> None:
    evaluation_contract = build_inspiration_v3_contract()
    call_a = strict_output_contract(
        "inspiration_image",
        "A",
        evaluation_contract=evaluation_contract,
        contract_profile=LATEST_INSPIRATION_CONTRACT_PROFILE,
    )
    call_b = strict_output_contract(
        "inspiration_image",
        "B",
        evaluation_contract=evaluation_contract,
        contract_profile=LATEST_INSPIRATION_CONTRACT_PROFILE,
    )

    merged = merged_strict_output_contract(
        "inspiration_image",
        ["A", "B"],
        evaluation_contract=evaluation_contract,
        name="inspiration_latest_single",
        description="灵感图最新单提示词固定字段",
        contract_profile=LATEST_INSPIRATION_CONTRACT_PROFILE,
    )
    parsed = parse_output_contract(merged)

    assert set(parsed.schema["properties"]) == (
        set(call_a["schema"]["properties"])
        | set(call_b["schema"]["properties"])
    )
    _assert_every_object_is_closed_and_required(parsed.schema)

            "watermark": "",
            "unrendered": "",
            "brand_trademark": "",
            "brand_name": "",
            "chinese": "",
            "film_ip": "",
            "celebrities": "",
            "game_ip": "",
            "other_ip": "",
            "qr_code": "",
            "landmark": "",
            "religion": "",
            "high_risk": [],
        },
    }


def _model_b_payload() -> dict[str, Any]:
    dimensions = (
        "model_detail",
        "material_rendering",
        "lighting",
        "design_trend",
        "visual_composition",
    )
    return {
        "aesthetic_score": 75,
        "aesthetic_evidence": ["主体、材质和背景关系完整"],
        "aesthetic_confidence": 0.8,
        "dimensions": {
            key: {"grade": 4, "evidence": [f"{key} 有直接可见证据"]}
            for key in dimensions
        },
        "overall_note": "整体完成度良好，局部细节仍可加强。",
    }


@pytest.mark.parametrize(
    ("category", "stage", "payload_factory"),
    [
        ("inspiration_image", "A", _inspiration_a_payload),
        ("inspiration_image", "B", _inspiration_b_payload),
        ("model_3d_su", "A", _model_a_payload),
        ("model_3d_su", "B", _model_b_payload),
    ],
)
def test_real_success_payload_shapes_validate_and_drift_fails_closed(
    category: str,
    stage: str,
    payload_factory,
) -> None:
    parsed = parse_output_contract(
        strict_output_contract(
            category,
            stage,
            evaluation_contract=_frozen_contract(category),
        )
    )
    payload = payload_factory()
    validate_output_payload(parsed, payload)

    missing = deepcopy(payload)
    missing.pop(next(iter(missing)))
    with pytest.raises(PromptOutputContractError) as missing_error:
        validate_output_payload(parsed, missing)
    assert missing_error.value.code == "structured_output_schema_mismatch"

    extra = deepcopy(payload)
    extra["unexpected"] = True
    with pytest.raises(PromptOutputContractError) as extra_error:
        validate_output_payload(parsed, extra)
    assert extra_error.value.code == "structured_output_schema_mismatch"


def test_nested_extra_and_wrong_enum_or_type_fail_closed() -> None:
    inspiration = parse_output_contract(
        strict_output_contract(
            "inspiration_image",
            "A",
            evaluation_contract=build_inspiration_v3_contract(),
        )
    )
    nested_extra = _inspiration_a_payload()
    nested_extra["decisive_evidence"]["unexpected"] = []
    with pytest.raises(PromptOutputContractError) as nested_error:
        validate_output_payload(inspiration, nested_extra)
    assert nested_error.value.code == "structured_output_schema_mismatch"

    wrong_enum = _inspiration_a_payload()
    wrong_enum["decision_status"] = "unknown"
    with pytest.raises(PromptOutputContractError) as enum_error:
        validate_output_payload(inspiration, wrong_enum)
    assert enum_error.value.code == "structured_output_schema_mismatch"

    wrong_type = _inspiration_a_payload()
    wrong_type["track_confidence"] = "0.9"
    with pytest.raises(PromptOutputContractError) as type_error:
        validate_output_payload(inspiration, wrong_type)
    assert type_error.value.code == "structured_output_schema_mismatch"


@pytest.mark.parametrize(
    "route",
    [
        "kg.knowledge_graph.domestic.whole.v1",
        "kg.knowledge_graph.domestic.single.v1",
        "kg.knowledge_graph.overseas.whole.v1",
        "kg.knowledge_graph.overseas.single.v1",
    ],
)
def test_knowledge_graph_reuses_authoritative_route_schema(route: str) -> None:
    authoritative = get_knowledge_graph_contract(route)
    assert authoritative is not None
    raw = strict_output_contract(
        "knowledge_graph",
        "A",
        evaluation_contract={"category_key": "knowledge_graph"},
        route=route,
    )
    parsed = parse_output_contract(raw)
    assert parsed.schema == authoritative.output_schema
    fixture_key = route.replace("kg.knowledge_graph", "kg")
    fixture = resolve_knowledge_graph_fixture(
        fixture_key,
        route,
        expected_asset_id="asset-001",
    )
    validate_output_payload(parsed, fixture)


def test_slim_prompt_removes_shape_boilerplate_but_keeps_business_rules() -> None:
    old_text = (BACKEND_PROMPTS / "inspiration_image_call_b_aesthetic_v5.txt").read_text()
    new_text = (
        BACKEND_PROMPTS / "inspiration_image_call_b_aesthetic_strict_v1.txt"
    ).read_text()
    contract = strict_output_contract(
        "inspiration_image",
        "B",
        evaluation_contract=build_inspiration_v3_contract(),
    )
    audit = prompt_slimming_audit(old_text, new_text, contract)
    assert audit.removed_markers >= {
        "只输出一个严格JSON对象",
        "顶层和逐维字段必须严格遵循",
    }
    assert audit.preserved_markers >= {
        "75分边界",
        "90分边界",
        "第五张才是待评对象",
        "相邻锚点比较",
    }
    assert audit.new_bytes < audit.old_bytes
    assert audit.schema_hash


def test_latest_inspiration_profile_freezes_full_redline_and_hard_defect_facts() -> None:
    contract = strict_output_contract(
        "inspiration_image",
        "A",
        evaluation_contract=build_inspiration_v3_contract(),
        contract_profile="inspiration-latest-v2-20260901",
    )
    schema = contract["schema"]
    assert set(schema["properties"]["redline_triggered"]["properties"]) == {
        "multi_image_collage",
        "large_area_watermark",
        "casual_photo",
        "screenshot",
        "upside_down_or_mirrored",
        "text_heavy",
        "qr_code_heavy",
        "unfinished_hand_sketch",
    }
    assert set(schema["properties"]["hard_defects"]["items"]["enum"]) == {
        "blurry_grayish",
        "careless_composition",
        "garish_color",
        "large_dead_black",
        "distorted_viewpoint",
        "fake_material",
        "fisheye_distortion",
        "invalid_black_border",
        "severe_color_cast",
    }
    reason_values = set(schema["properties"]["reason"]["items"]["enum"])
    assert {"是多拼图", "有大面积水印", "是颠倒图", "是未完成手绘草图"} <= (
        reason_values
    )


def test_latest_inspiration_b_schema_keeps_runtime_fields_and_describes_doc_dimensions() -> None:
    contract = strict_output_contract(
        "inspiration_image",
        "B",
        evaluation_contract=build_inspiration_v3_contract(),
        contract_profile="inspiration-latest-v2-20260901",
    )
    dimensions = contract["schema"]["properties"]["dimensions"]["properties"]
    properties = contract["schema"]["properties"]
    assert properties["contract_version"]["const"] == LATEST_FOUNDATION_VERSION
    # 紧凑形状：来源扣分只列命中项 [{key, evidence}]
    assert properties["source_deductions"]["type"] == "array"
    assert set(properties["source_deductions"]["items"]["properties"]["key"]["enum"]) == {
        "webpage_url",
        "plain_background_single_object",
        "low_quality_or_old_grayscale",
    }
    assert set(dimensions) == set(DIMENSION_KEYS)
    descriptions = " ".join(item["description"] for item in dimensions.values())
    for source_dimension in (
        "视觉结构",
        "色彩美学",
        "情绪表达",
        "设计美感",
        "原创性",
        "潮流度",
    ):
        assert source_dimension in descriptions


def test_latest_prompts_are_compact_and_keep_business_semantics_out_of_json_shape() -> None:
    call_a = (BACKEND_PROMPTS / "inspiration_image_call_a_strict_v2.txt").read_text()
    call_b = (
        BACKEND_PROMPTS / "inspiration_image_call_b_aesthetic_strict_v2.txt"
    ).read_text()
    for marker in (
        "多拼图",
        "大面积水印",
        "随手拍",
        "截图",
        "颠倒",
        "40%",
        "二维码",
        "未完成手绘草图",
    ):
        assert marker in call_a
    for marker in (
        "模糊发灰",
        "构图敷衍",
        "艳俗失真",
        "大面积死黑",
        "视角畸形",
        "材质虚假",
        "鱼眼畸变",
        "无效黑边",
        "严重偏色",
    ):
        assert marker in call_a
    assert "80分边界" in call_b
    assert "90分边界" in call_b
    assert "75分边界" not in call_b
    assert "L5" in call_b and "调用 A" in call_b
    assert "网页网址" in call_b
    assert "纯背景单物体" in call_b
    assert "低质或老旧灰度图" in call_b
    assert "每项扣10分" in call_b
    assert len(call_a.encode("utf-8")) < 3200
    assert len(call_b.encode("utf-8")) < len(
        (BACKEND_PROMPTS / "inspiration_image_call_b_aesthetic_v5.txt")
        .read_bytes()
    )
    for text in (call_a, call_b):
        assert "```" not in text
        assert not any(line.lstrip().startswith(("{", "[")) for line in text.splitlines())


@pytest.mark.parametrize(
    "prompt_name",
    [
        "inspiration_image_call_a_strict_v1.txt",
        "inspiration_image_call_b_aesthetic_strict_v1.txt",
        "inspiration_image_call_a_strict_v2.txt",
        "inspiration_image_call_b_aesthetic_strict_v2.txt",
        "inspiration_image_call_a_strict_v3.txt",
        "inspiration_image_call_b_aesthetic_strict_v3.txt",
        "inspiration_latest_single_v2.txt",
        "model_3d_su_call_a_strict_v1.txt",
        "model_3d_su_call_b_strict_v1.txt",
    ],
)
def test_strict_prompt_has_no_serialized_schema_or_sample_json(prompt_name: str) -> None:
    text = (BACKEND_PROMPTS / prompt_name).read_text()
    assert "```" not in text
    assert "必须返回以下结构" not in text
    assert "输出结构" not in text
    assert "顶层和逐维字段必须严格遵循" not in text
    assert not any(line.lstrip().startswith(("{", "[")) for line in text.splitlines())


@pytest.mark.parametrize(
    ("category", "stage", "route"),
    [
        ("unknown", "A", None),
        ("inspiration_image", "C", None),
        ("knowledge_graph", "A", None),
        ("knowledge_graph", "A", "kg.knowledge_graph.missing.v1"),
    ],
)
def test_unknown_contract_selection_fails_stably(
    category: str,
    stage: str,
    route: str | None,
) -> None:
    with pytest.raises(ValueError) as error:

            category,

            route=route,
        )
