def test_compact_evidence_shapes_expand_to_canonical_before_validation() -> None:
    from app.inspiration_aesthetic_foundation import (
        LATEST_FOUNDATION_VERSION as _LATEST,
        expand_compact_aesthetic_output,
        validate_aesthetic_output,
    )
    from app.schema_adapter import expand_compact_redline_evidence

    compact_b = {
        "contract_version": _LATEST,
        "aesthetic_score": 71,
        "dimensions": {
            key: {"grade": 4, "evidence": "一句话证据", "shortcomings": ""}
            for key in DIMENSION_KEYS
        },
        "overall_evidence": "接近 L2 锚点",
        "confidence": 0.8,
        "source_deductions": [{"key": "webpage_url", "evidence": "画面右下角有域名"}],
    }
    expanded = expand_compact_aesthetic_output(compact_b)
    assert expanded["dimensions"][DIMENSION_KEYS[0]]["evidence"] == ["一句话证据"]
    assert expanded["dimensions"][DIMENSION_KEYS[0]]["shortcomings"] == []
    assert expanded["overall_evidence"] == ["接近 L2 锚点"]
    assert expanded["source_deductions"]["webpage_url"] == {
        "hit": True,
        "evidence": ["画面右下角有域名"],
    }
    assert expanded["source_deductions"]["plain_background_single_object"] == {
        "hit": False,
        "evidence": [],
    }
    normalized = validate_aesthetic_output(compact_b)
    assert normalized["source_deductions"]["webpage_url"]["hit"] is True
    # 空证据串展开后仍被判非法（fail-closed 语义不变）
    broken = dict(compact_b)
    broken["dimensions"] = {
        key: {"grade": 4, "evidence": "", "shortcomings": ""} for key in DIMENSION_KEYS
    }
    with pytest.raises(Exception):
        validate_aesthetic_output(broken)

    keys = ("screenshot", "text_heavy")
    compact_a = {
        "redline_triggered": [{"key": "text_heavy", "evidence": "文字块累加超过四成"}],
        "hard_defects": [],
    }
    assert expand_compact_redline_evidence(compact_a, redline_keys=keys) == {
        "redline_triggered": {"screenshot": [], "text_heavy": ["文字块累加超过四成"]},
        "hard_defects": [],
    }
    canonical = {"redline_triggered": {"screenshot": [], "text_heavy": []}, "hard_defects": []}
    assert expand_compact_redline_evidence(canonical, redline_keys=keys) == canonical
    unknown = expand_compact_redline_evidence(
        {"redline_triggered": [{"key": "nope", "evidence": "x"}]}, redline_keys=keys
    )
    assert unknown["redline_triggered"]["__unknown__"] == ["nope"]

===== FILE backend/tests/test_strict_prompt_release.py =====
from __future__ import annotations

import json

import pytest
from sqlalchemy import create_engine, func, select
from sqlalchemy.orm import Session

from app.audit import canonical_json
from app.database import Base
from app.models import (
    BaselineRegressionRun,
    BaselineSet,
    EvaluationCategoryProfile,
    ModelConfig,
    ModelRegistryEntry,
    PromptVersion,
    RuleModuleVersion,
    WorkflowNodeAssetBinding,
    WorkflowVersion,
)
from app.native_workflow_migration import ensure_native_category_workflows
from app.seed import seed_defaults
from app.vision_input_policy import SEED_20_PROFILE
from app.workflow_registry import persisted_workflow_manifest


SEED_20_CURRENT_MODEL = "doubao-seed-2-0-lite-260428"
EXPECTED_VERSIONS = {
    "inspiration_image": "inspiration-strict-high-v11-20260902",
    "model_3d_su": "model-3d-su-strict-high-v1-20260901",
    "knowledge_graph": "kg-native-strict-high-v1-20260901",
}

LATEST_REDLINE_KEYS = {
    "multi_image_collage",
    "large_area_watermark",
    "casual_photo",
    "screenshot",
    "upside_down_or_mirrored",
    "text_heavy",
    "qr_code_heavy",
    "unfinished_hand_sketch",
}
LATEST_HARD_DEFECTS = {
    "blurry_grayish",
    "careless_composition",
    "garish_color",
    "large_dead_black",
    "distorted_viewpoint",
    "fake_material",
    "fisheye_distortion",
    "invalid_black_border",
    "severe_color_cast",
}
_INSPIRATION_EXPECTED_SKIP_ROLES = (
    "classification",
    "grading",
    "level_matching",
)


def _prepare_database(tmp_path):
    engine = create_engine(f"sqlite:///{tmp_path / 'strict-release.db'}")
    Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
    with Session(engine) as db:
        db.add(
            EvaluationCategoryProfile(
                category_key="inspiration_image",
                display_name="灵感图",
                status="active",
                allowed_mime_types_json='["image/jpeg","image/png"]',
                preprocess_config_json='{"preprocess":"image"}',
                pipeline_config_json="{}",
                created_by="system:test",
            )
        )
        seed_defaults(db)
        profile = canonical_json(dict(SEED_20_PROFILE))
        for row in db.scalars(select(ModelConfig)).all():
            row.model_id = SEED_20_CURRENT_MODEL
            row.vision_capability_profile_json = profile
        for row in db.scalars(select(ModelRegistryEntry)).all():
            row.model_id = SEED_20_CURRENT_MODEL
            row.vision_capability_profile_json = profile
        db.flush()
        ensure_native_category_workflows(
            db,
            "inspiration_image",
            actor="system:test-source",
        )
        db.commit()
    return engine


def _counts(db: Session) -> tuple[int, int, int]:
    return (
        db.scalar(select(func.count(PromptVersion.id))) or 0,
        db.scalar(select(func.count(WorkflowVersion.id))) or 0,
        db.scalar(select(func.count(WorkflowNodeAssetBinding.id))) or 0,
    )


def test_evaluation_contract_accepts_tamper_evident_projection_snapshot(
    tmp_path,
) -> None:
    from app.strict_prompt_release import _evaluation_contract, _sha256

    engine = _prepare_database(tmp_path)
    contract = {
        "schema_version": "evaluation-category-profile-v3",
        "category_key": "inspiration_image",
        "spec_version": "latest-live-contract",
    }
    workflow_fields = {
        "category_key": "inspiration_image",
        "contract": contract,
    }
    manifest = {
        "resource_policy": {
            "legacy_projection_snapshot": {
                "schema_version": "legacy-projection-workflow-fields-v1",
                "available": True,
                "workflow_fields": workflow_fields,
                "workflow_fields_hash": _sha256(workflow_fields),
            }
        }
    }
    try:
        with Session(engine) as db:
            resolved = _evaluation_contract(
                db,
                category_key="inspiration_image",
                source_manifest=manifest,
            )
            assert resolved == contract
            assert resolved is not contract
    finally:
        engine.dispose()


def test_evaluation_contract_rejects_tampered_projection_snapshot(tmp_path) -> None:
    from app.strict_prompt_release import (
        StrictPromptReleaseError,
        _evaluation_contract,
    )

    engine = _prepare_database(tmp_path)
    manifest = {
        "resource_policy": {
            "legacy_projection_snapshot": {
                "schema_version": "legacy-projection-workflow-fields-v1",
                "available": True,
                "workflow_fields": {
                    "category_key": "inspiration_image",
                    "contract": {
                        "category_key": "inspiration_image",
                        "schema_version": "evaluation-category-profile-v3",
                    },
                },
                "workflow_fields_hash": "0" * 64,
            }
        }
    }
    try:
        with Session(engine) as db:
            with pytest.raises(
                StrictPromptReleaseError,
                match="缺少唯一历史评测合同",
            ):
                _evaluation_contract(
                    db,
                    category_key="inspiration_image",
                    source_manifest=manifest,
                )
    finally:
        engine.dispose()


def test_source_workflow_skips_newer_non_doubao_candidate(tmp_path) -> None:
    from app.strict_prompt_release import _source_workflow

    engine = _prepare_database(tmp_path)
    try:
        with Session(engine) as db:
            source = _source_workflow(
                db,
                category_key="inspiration_image",
                release_version=EXPECTED_VERSIONS["inspiration_image"],
            )
            runtime_model = ModelConfig(
                name="隔离 Qwen 运行模型",
                provider="local",
                base_url="http://127.0.0.1:9999",
                model_id="qwen-test-model",
            )
            db.add(runtime_model)
            db.flush()
            registry_model = ModelRegistryEntry(
                role="main",
                name="隔离 Qwen 注册模型",
                provider="local",
                protocol="openai_chat",
                base_url="http://127.0.0.1:9999",
                api_path="/chat/completions",
                model_id="qwen-test-model",
                source_model_config_id=runtime_model.id,
                created_by="system:test",
            )
            db.add(registry_model)
            db.flush()

            nodes = json.loads(source.step_manifest_json)
            for node in nodes:
                if node.get("kind") == "prompt":
                    node["config"]["model_registry_id"] = registry_model.id
            newer = WorkflowVersion(
                workflow_definition_id=source.workflow_definition_id,
                version="newer-qwen-source",
                status="active",
                workflow_schema_version=source.workflow_schema_version,
                step_manifest_json=canonical_json(nodes),
                edge_manifest_json=source.edge_manifest_json,
                input_schema_json=source.input_schema_json,
                output_schema_json=source.output_schema_json,
                resource_policy_json=source.resource_policy_json,
                canonical_hash="f" * 64,
                validation_report_json=canonical_json({"ok": True}),
                created_by="system:test",
            )
            db.add(newer)
            db.flush()
            baseline_set = BaselineSet(
                category_key="inspiration_image",
                name="隔离 Qwen 已完成回归集",
                description="",
                default_expected_level="L1",
                fingerprint="e" * 64,
                created_by="system:test",
            )
            db.add(baseline_set)
            db.flush()
            db.add(
                BaselineRegressionRun(
                    baseline_set_id=baseline_set.id,
                    sequence_no=1,
                    workflow_version_id=newer.id,
                    execution_engine="native_workflow",
                    category_key="inspiration_image",
                    strategy_snapshot_json="{}",
                    execution_snapshot_json="{}",
                    correction_contract_json="{}",
                    baseline_set_fingerprint=baseline_set.fingerprint,
                    status="completed",
                    total=1,
                    completed=1,
                    valid_predictions=1,
                    failed=0,
                    metrics_json="{}",
                    created_by="system:test",
                )
            )
            db.flush()

            resolved = _source_workflow(
                db,
                category_key="inspiration_image",
                release_version=EXPECTED_VERSIONS["inspiration_image"],
            )
            assert resolved.id == source.id
    finally:
        engine.dispose()


def test_preview_is_zero_write_and_reports_all_three_release_plans(tmp_path) -> None:
    from app.strict_prompt_release import materialize_strict_vision_candidates

    engine = _prepare_database(tmp_path)
    try:
        with Session(engine) as db:
            before = _counts(db)
            receipt = materialize_strict_vision_candidates(
                db,
                actor="system:strict-release-test",
                apply=False,
            )
            assert receipt.mode == "preview"
            assert _counts(db) == before
            assert {item.category_key: item.version for item in receipt.workflows} == (
                EXPECTED_VERSIONS
            )
            assert all(item.status == "candidate" for item in receipt.workflows)
            assert all(item.candidate_workflow_id is None for item in receipt.workflows)
            assert all(len(item.plan_hash) == 64 for item in receipt.workflows)
            assert all(item.rollback_workflow_id > 0 for item in receipt.workflows)
    finally:
        engine.dispose()


def test_apply_is_idempotent_and_preserves_source_workflow_hashes(tmp_path) -> None:
    from app.strict_prompt_release import materialize_strict_vision_candidates

    engine = _prepare_database(tmp_path)
    try:
        with Session(engine) as db:
            source_hashes = {
                row.id: row.canonical_hash
                for row in db.scalars(
                    select(WorkflowVersion).where(WorkflowVersion.status == "active")
                ).all()
            }
            first = materialize_strict_vision_candidates(
                db,
                actor="system:strict-release-test",
                apply=True,
            )
            db.commit()
            counts_after_first = _counts(db)
            second = materialize_strict_vision_candidates(
                db,
                actor="system:strict-release-test",
                apply=True,
            )
            db.commit()

            assert first == second
            assert _counts(db) == counts_after_first
            assert {
                workflow_id: db.get(WorkflowVersion, workflow_id).canonical_hash
                for workflow_id in source_hashes
            } == source_hashes
            assert all(item.status == "candidate" for item in first.workflows)
            assert all(item.candidate_workflow_id for item in first.workflows)
            assert all(item.canonical_hash for item in first.workflows)
            assert all(item.rollback_workflow_id for item in first.workflows)

            for item in first.workflows:
                row = db.get(WorkflowVersion, item.candidate_workflow_id)
                assert row is not None
                assert row.version == EXPECTED_VERSIONS[item.category_key]
                assert row.status == "draft"
                report = json.loads(row.validation_report_json)
                assert report["candidate"] is True
                assert report["activated"] is False
    finally:
        engine.dispose()


def test_candidate_prompts_are_strict_high_high_current_model_and_logical_only(
    tmp_path,
) -> None:
    from app.strict_prompt_release import (
        STRICT_HIGH_POLICY,
        materialize_strict_vision_candidates,
    )

    engine = _prepare_database(tmp_path)
    try:
        with Session(engine) as db:
            receipt = materialize_strict_vision_candidates(
                db,
                actor="system:strict-release-test",
                apply=True,
            )
            db.commit()

            prompt_counts = {
                "inspiration_image": 2,
                "model_3d_su": 2,
                "knowledge_graph": 4,
            }
            for item in receipt.workflows:
                workflow = db.get(WorkflowVersion, item.candidate_workflow_id)
                assert workflow is not None
                manifest = persisted_workflow_manifest(workflow)
                policy = manifest["resource_policy"]
                assert policy["candidate_release"]["status"] == "candidate"
                assert policy["physical_production_projection_enabled"] is False
                prompt_nodes = [
                    node for node in manifest["nodes"] if node["kind"] == "prompt"
                ]
                assert len(prompt_nodes) == prompt_counts[item.category_key]
                for node in prompt_nodes:
                    config = node["config"]
                    assert config["thinking_level"] == "none"
                    assert config["thinking_enabled"] is False
                    contract = config["output_contract"]
                    assert contract["mode"] == "json_schema"
                    assert contract["strict"] is True
                    assert contract["schema"]["additionalProperties"] is False
                    assert config["structured_output"] is True
                    assert config["vision_input_policy"] == STRICT_HIGH_POLICY
                    assert len(config["vision_policy_hash"]) == 64
                    assert config["model_snapshot"]["model_id"] == (
                        SEED_20_CURRENT_MODEL
                    )
                    assert config["vision_capability_snapshot"]["profile_key"] == (
                        "volcengine-seed-2.0-v20260901"
                    )
                    prompt = db.get(PromptVersion, config["prompt_version_id"])
                    assert prompt is not None
                    assert prompt.status == "draft"
                    assert prompt.rollback_prompt_id is not None
                    assert prompt.source == "strict_release"
                for node in manifest["nodes"]:
                    if node["kind"] not in {
                        "upstream_connector",
                        "downstream_projection",
                    }:
                        continue
                    config = node["config"]
                    assert config["connector_key"].startswith("logical.")
                    if node["kind"] == "downstream_projection":
                        assert config["completion_mode"] == "continue"

            kg = next(
                item for item in receipt.workflows if item.category_key == "knowledge_graph"
            )
            kg_manifest = persisted_workflow_manifest(
                db.get(WorkflowVersion, kg.candidate_workflow_id)
            )
            kg_contracts = {
                node["config"]["output_contract_key"]: node["config"][
                    "output_contract"
                ]
                for node in kg_manifest["nodes"]
                if node["kind"] == "prompt"
            }
            assert set(kg_contracts) == {
                "kg.knowledge_graph.domestic.whole.v1",
                "kg.knowledge_graph.domestic.single.v1",
                "kg.knowledge_graph.overseas.whole.v1",
                "kg.knowledge_graph.overseas.single.v1",
            }
            assert all(
                set(contract["schema"]["required"])
                == set(contract["schema"]["properties"])
                for contract in kg_contracts.values()
            )
    finally:
        engine.dispose()


def test_inspiration_v2_candidate_freezes_latest_terminal_routes(tmp_path) -> None:
    from app.strict_prompt_release import materialize_strict_vision_candidates

    engine = _prepare_database(tmp_path)
    try:
        with Session(engine) as db:
            receipt = materialize_strict_vision_candidates(
                db,
                actor="system:strict-release-test",
                apply=True,
            )
            db.commit()
            item = next(
                row for row in receipt.workflows if row.category_key == "inspiration_image"
            )
            workflow = db.get(WorkflowVersion, item.candidate_workflow_id)
            assert workflow is not None
            manifest = persisted_workflow_manifest(workflow)
            nodes = {row["node_instance_id"]: row for row in manifest["nodes"]}

            prompt_a = nodes["prompt_a"]["config"]
            assert prompt_a["execution_role"] == "classification"
            rules = {row["rule_key"]: row for row in prompt_a["short_circuit_rules"]}
            assert set(rules) == {
                "inspiration-redline-l5-v2",
                "inspiration-class-two-l3-v2",
                "inspiration-class-three-l4-v2",
                "inspiration-hard-defect-l4-v2",
            }
            assert [
                rules[key]["target_level"]
                for key in (
                    "inspiration-redline-l5-v2",
                    "inspiration-class-two-l3-v2",
                    "inspiration-class-three-l4-v2",
                    "inspiration-hard-defect-l4-v2",
                )
            ] == ["L5", "L3", "L4", "L4"]
            assert [
                rules[key]["priority"]
                for key in (
                    "inspiration-redline-l5-v2",
                    "inspiration-class-two-l3-v2",
                    "inspiration-class-three-l4-v2",
                    "inspiration-hard-defect-l4-v2",
                )
            ] == [1000, 800, 900, 900]  # 命中优先级 L5 > L4 > L3
            assert all(
                rule["skip_roles"]
                == ["classification", "grading", "level_matching"]
                for rule in rules.values()
            )

            redline_any = rules["inspiration-redline-l5-v2"]["when"]["all"][1][
                "any"
            ]
            assert {
                row["path"].removeprefix("output.redline_triggered.")
                for row in redline_any
            } == LATEST_REDLINE_KEYS
            defect_any = rules["inspiration-hard-defect-l4-v2"]["when"]["all"][1][
                "any"
            ]
            assert len(defect_any) == len(LATEST_HARD_DEFECTS)
            assert all(row["value"] == sorted(LATEST_HARD_DEFECTS) for row in defect_any)

            assert nodes["prompt_b"]["config"]["execution_role"] == "grading"
            assert nodes["score_level_matcher_7"]["config"]["execution_role"] == (
                "level_matching"
            )
            for node_id in (
                "eligibility_redline_1",
                "track_routing_2",
            ):
                assert nodes[node_id]["config"]["execution_role"] == "classification"
            for node_id in (
                "media_adjustment_3",
                "quality_cap_exception_4",
                "dimension_scoring_5",
                "anchor_calibration_6",
            ):
                assert nodes[node_id]["config"]["execution_role"] == "grading"

            media_node = nodes["media_adjustment_3"]
            media_version = db.get(
                RuleModuleVersion,
                media_node["config"]["rule_module_version_id"],
            )
            assert media_version is not None
            media_content = json.loads(media_version.content_json)
            assert media_content["enabled"] is True
            assert media_content["media_penalty_enabled"] is True
            assert media_content["penalties"] == {
                "real_photo": 0,
                "render_3d": -5,
                "ai_image": -10,
                "other": -10,
            }

            matcher_node = nodes["score_level_matcher_7"]
            matcher_version = db.get(
                RuleModuleVersion,
                matcher_node["config"]["rule_module_version_id"],
            )
            assert matcher_version is not None
            matcher_content = json.loads(matcher_version.content_json)
            assert matcher_content["legacy_level_thresholds"] == [
                {"min_score": 90, "level": "L1"},
                {"min_score": 80, "level": "L2"},
                {"min_score": 60, "level": "L3"},
                {"min_score": 0, "level": "L4"},
            ]
            assert matcher_content["legacy_score_thresholds"] == [
                {"min_score": 90, "level": "L1"},
                {"min_score": 80, "level": "L2"},
                {"min_score": 60, "level": "L3"},
                {"min_score": 0, "level": "L4"},
            ]
            assert {
                row["level"]: row.get("min_score")
                for row in matcher_content["levels"]
                if row["enabled"]
            } == {"L1": 90, "L2": 80, "L3": 60, "L4": 0}

            assert {rule.asset_key for rule in item.rules} == {
                "media_adjustment",
                "score_level_matcher",
            }
            assert all(rule.candidate_rule_module_id for rule in item.rules)
            assert all(
                rule.rollback_rule_module_id == rule.source_rule_module_id
                for rule in item.rules
            )
    finally:
        engine.dispose()


def test_inspiration_v2_terminal_routes_resolve_and_skip_call_b(tmp_path) -> None:
    from app.strict_prompt_release import materialize_strict_vision_candidates
    from app.workflow_short_circuit import (
        downstream_skipped_nodes,
        resolve_short_circuit,
    )

    engine = _prepare_database(tmp_path)
    try:
        with Session(engine) as db:
            receipt = materialize_strict_vision_candidates(
                db,
                actor="system:strict-release-test",
                apply=True,
            )
            db.commit()
            item = next(
                row for row in receipt.workflows if row.category_key == "inspiration_image"
            )
            workflow = db.get(WorkflowVersion, item.candidate_workflow_id)
            assert workflow is not None
            manifest = persisted_workflow_manifest(workflow)
            prompt_a = next(
                row for row in manifest["nodes"] if row["node_instance_id"] == "prompt_a"
            )
            rules = prompt_a["config"]["short_circuit_rules"]
            flags = {key: False for key in LATEST_REDLINE_KEYS}

            def decide(**updates):
                payload = {
                    "decision_status": "complete",
                    "redline_triggered": dict(flags),
                    "track_classification": "class_one",
                    "hard_defects": [],
                    **updates,
                }
                return resolve_short_circuit(
                    rules,
                    {"output": payload},
                    ["L1", "L2", "L3", "L4", "L5"],
                )

            redline = dict(flags)
            redline["multi_image_collage"] = True
            assert decide(
                redline_triggered=redline,
                track_classification="class_two",
                hard_defects=["garish_color"],
            ).target_level == "L5"
            assert decide(
                track_classification="class_two",
                hard_defects=["garish_color"],
            ).target_level == "L4"  # 硬伤 L4 严于二类 L3，L4 胜出
            assert decide(track_classification="class_three").target_level == "L4"
            assert decide(hard_defects=["severe_color_cast"]).target_level == "L4"
            assert decide() is None
            assert decide(
                decision_status="uncertain",
                redline_triggered=redline,
            ) is None

            skipped = downstream_skipped_nodes(
                manifest,
                source_node_id="prompt_a",
                skip_roles=_INSPIRATION_EXPECTED_SKIP_ROLES,
            )
            assert "prompt_b" in skipped
            assert "score_level_matcher_7" in skipped
            assert "downstream" not in skipped
    finally:
        engine.dispose()


def test_materialize_strict_candidates_can_be_scoped_to_one_category(tmp_path) -> None:
    from app.strict_prompt_release import (
        StrictPromptReleaseError,
        materialize_strict_vision_candidates,

        )
    assert exc.value.code == "strict_release_category_unknown"


def test_freeze_projects_from_pinned_legacy_revision(tmp_path) -> None:
    """带 migration_identity 的候选按其源修订投影，而不是被 active 修订覆盖。"""
    from copy import deepcopy

    from app.category_evaluation_v3_revisions import (
        RevisionArtifacts,
        create_candidate_revision,
        ensure_projected_revision,
    )
    from app.models import CategoryEvaluationV3Config
    from app.workflow_v3 import freeze_component_references

    engine = _prepare_database(tmp_path)
    with Session(engine) as db:
        projected = db.scalar(
            select(CategoryEvaluationV3Config).where(
                CategoryEvaluationV3Config.category_key == "inspiration_image"
            )
        )
        assert projected is not None
        current = ensure_projected_revision(db, projected)
        contract = json.loads(projected.contract_json)
        contract["image_defects_enabled"] = False
        candidate, created = create_candidate_revision(
            db,
            projected,
            parent_revision_id=current.id,
            artifacts=RevisionArtifacts(
                display_name="pinned-test",
                contract=contract,
                classification_map=json.loads(projected.classification_map_json),
                subcategory_dimensions=json.loads(
                    projected.subcategory_dimensions_json
                ),
            ),
            expected_projected_revision=projected.revision,
            expected_projected_hash=projected.contract_hash,
            actor="system:test",
        )
        assert created is True
        db.flush()
        source = db.scalar(
            select(WorkflowVersion).where(
                WorkflowVersion.version == f"native-r{current.revision}"
            )
        )
        assert source is not None
        manifest = persisted_workflow_manifest(source)

        pinned = deepcopy(manifest)
        pinned["resource_policy"]["migration_identity"] = {
            "schema_version": "native-category-workflow-migration-v1",
            "legacy_revision_id": candidate.id,
            "legacy_revision": candidate.revision,
        }
        frozen = freeze_component_references(db, pinned)
        proj = frozen["resource_policy"]["legacy_projection_snapshot"]["workflow_fields"]["contract"]
        assert proj.get("image_defects_enabled") is False

        unpinned = deepcopy(manifest)
        unpinned["resource_policy"].pop("migration_identity", None)
        frozen2 = freeze_component_references(db, unpinned)
        proj2 = frozen2["resource_policy"]["legacy_projection_snapshot"]["workflow_fields"]["contract"]
        assert proj2.get("image_defects_enabled") is None

===== FILE backend/tests/test_structured_baseline_sets.py =====
from __future__ import annotations

import json
from contextlib import contextmanager

from fastapi.testclient import TestClient
from sqlalchemy import create_engine, select
from sqlalchemy.orm import Session
from sqlalchemy.pool import StaticPool

from app.baseline_regression import baseline_set_fingerprint
from app.database import Base, get_db
from app.knowledge_graph_contracts import resolve_knowledge_graph_fixture
from app.main import app, current_user
from app.models import Asset, BaselineSetItem, EvaluationCategoryProfile, User


@contextmanager
def _structured_baseline_client():
    engine = create_engine(
        "sqlite://",
        connect_args={"check_same_thread": False},
        poolclass=StaticPool,
    )
    Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
    db = Session(engine, expire_on_commit=False)
    user = User(is_admin=True, role="admin", username="structured-truth-operator", password_hash="unused")
    profile = EvaluationCategoryProfile(
        category_key="knowledge_graph",
        display_name="知识图谱打标",
        status="active",
        allowed_mime_types_json='["image/jpeg"]',
        preprocess_config_json='{"preprocess":"image"}',
        pipeline_config_json="{}",
        created_by=user.username,
    )
    legacy_profile = EvaluationCategoryProfile(
        category_key="model_3d_su",
        display_name="3D & SU 模型",
        status="active",
        allowed_mime_types_json='["image/jpeg"]',
        preprocess_config_json='{"preprocess":"image"}',
        pipeline_config_json="{}",
        created_by=user.username,
    )
    design_case_profile = EvaluationCategoryProfile(
        category_key="design_case",
        display_name="设计案例",
        status="active",
        allowed_mime_types_json='["image/jpeg"]',
        preprocess_config_json='{"preprocess":"image"}',
        pipeline_config_json="{}",
        created_by=user.username,
    )
    asset = Asset(
        original_name="kg-domestic-whole.jpg",
        stored_name="kg-domestic-whole.jpg",
        mime_type="image/jpeg",
        size_bytes=128,
        sha256="a" * 64,
        category_key="knowledge_graph",
        status="uploaded",
    )
    db.add_all([user, profile, legacy_profile, design_case_profile, asset])
    db.commit()

    app.dependency_overrides[get_db] = lambda: (yield db)
    app.dependency_overrides[current_user] = lambda: user
    client = TestClient(app)
    try:
        yield client, db, asset
    finally:
        app.dependency_overrides.clear()
        db.close()
        engine.dispose()


def test_knowledge_graph_truth_contracts_are_operator_readable() -> None:
    with _structured_baseline_client() as (client, _db, _asset):
        response = client.get("/api/baseline-truth-contracts/knowledge_graph")

        assert response.status_code == 200
        payload = response.json()
        assert payload["category_key"] == "knowledge_graph"
        assert payload["truth_kind"] == "structured_fields"
        assert [item["display_name"] for item in payload["items"]] == [
            "国内 · 整体图",
            "国内 · 单体图",
            "海外 · 整体图",
            "海外 · 单体图",
        ]
        domestic_whole = payload["items"][0]
        assert domestic_whole["contract_key"] == (
            "kg.knowledge_graph.domestic.whole.v1"
        )
        assert domestic_whole["identity_field"] == "ll_id"
        assert domestic_whole["identity_label"] == "国内素材 ID"
        assert domestic_whole["is_single"] == 0
        assert domestic_whole["fields"][0] == {
            "key": "ll_id",
            "label": "国内素材 ID",
            "description": "原样返回国内链路传入的 ll_id",
            "type": "string",
            "required": True,
            "fixed_value": None,
        }
        assert domestic_whole["fields"][1]["fixed_value"] == 0

        legacy = client.get("/api/baseline-truth-contracts/model_3d_su")
        assert legacy.status_code == 200
        assert legacy.json() == {
            "category_key": "model_3d_su",
            "truth_kind": "level",
            "items": [],
        }


def test_operator_can_freeze_knowledge_graph_structured_truth() -> None:
    with _structured_baseline_client() as (client, db, asset):
        contract_key = "kg.knowledge_graph.domestic.whole.v1"
        expected = resolve_knowledge_graph_fixture(
            "kg.domestic.whole.v1",
            contract_key,
            expected_asset_id="ll-domestic-001",
        )

        response = client.post(
            "/api/baseline-sets",
            json={
                "name": "知识图谱国内整体结构化真值",
                "category_key": "knowledge_graph",
                "default_expected_level": None,
                "items": [
                    {
                        "asset_id": asset.id,
                        "truth_contract_key": contract_key,
                        "expected_result": expected,
                    }
                ],
            },
        )

        assert response.status_code == 200, response.text
        summary = response.json()
        assert summary["truth_kind"] == "structured_fields"
        assert summary["default_expected_level"] is None

        item = db.scalar(select(BaselineSetItem))
        assert item is not None
        assert item.expected_level is None
        assert json.loads(item.expected_result_json or "{}") == expected
        assert item.truth_contract_key == contract_key
        snapshot = json.loads(item.asset_snapshot_json)
        assert snapshot["schema_version"] == "baseline-asset-v2"
        assert snapshot["workflow_input"] == {
            "source": "domestic",
            "is_single": 0,
            "ll_id": "ll-domestic-001",
            "fixture_key": "kg.domestic.whole.v1",
        }

        detail = client.get(f"/api/baseline-sets/{summary['id']}")
        assert detail.status_code == 200
        frozen = detail.json()["items"][0]
        assert frozen["truth_kind"] == "structured_fields"
        assert frozen["expected_level"] is None
        assert frozen["expected_result"] == expected
        assert frozen["truth_contract_key"] == contract_key
        assert frozen["workflow_input"] == snapshot["workflow_input"]
        assert frozen["truth_source"] == "baseline_import"
        assert frozen["truth_revision"] == 0
        assert frozen["truth_revision_id"] is None
        assert len(frozen["truth_hash"]) == 64
        assert frozen["pending_truth_field_count"] == sum(
            value == "" for value in expected.values()
        )
        assert frozen["original_truth"]["expected_result"] == expected
        assert frozen["effective_truth"]["expected_result"] == expected
        assert frozen["effective_truth"]["revision"] == 0
        assert frozen["effective_truth"]["canonical_hash"] == frozen["truth_hash"]


def test_operator_can_freeze_strict_design_case_truth_with_all_image_assets() -> None:
    from app.baseline_truth import DESIGN_CASE_TRUTH_CONTRACT

    with _structured_baseline_client() as (client, db, _asset):
        first = Asset(
            original_name="case-first.jpg",
            stored_name="case-first.jpg",
            mime_type="image/jpeg",
            size_bytes=128,
            sha256="b" * 64,
            category_key="design_case",
            status="uploaded",
        )
        second = Asset(
            original_name="case-second.jpg",
            stored_name="case-second.jpg",
            mime_type="image/jpeg",
            size_bytes=256,
            sha256="c" * 64,
            category_key="design_case",
            status="uploaded",
        )
        db.add_all([first, second])
        db.commit()

        catalog = client.get("/api/baseline-truth-contracts/design_case")
        assert catalog.status_code == 200
        case_contract = catalog.json()["items"][0]
        assert case_contract["contract_key"] == (
            DESIGN_CASE_TRUTH_CONTRACT
        )
        assert case_contract["contract_kind"] == "design_case"
        assert case_contract["identity_field"] == "case_cloud_id"
        assert case_contract["workflow_input_required"] is True
        assert [field["key"] for field in case_contract["fields"]] == [
            "case_cloud_id",
            "level",
            "final_score",
            "case_review.score",
            "case_review.reason",
            "case_review.seo_title",
            "case_review.category_one",
            "case_review.category_two",
            "case_review.style",
            "case_review.matcraft",
            "case_review.designtactic",
            "case_review.spaceshape",
            "case_review.clientInfo",
            "case_review.projattr",
            "case_review.designer",
            "case_review.region_type",
            "case_review.location_detail",
            "case_review.scale",
            "case_review.dtime",
            "case_review.btime",
        ]

        workflow_input = {
            "case": {
                "cloud_id": "case-100",
                "project_id": "project-100",
                "case_name": "示例案例",
                "case_text": "冻结案例说明",
                "quality_score": 5,
                "time_score": 3,
                "filter_threshold": 70,
                "images": [
                    {
                        "cloud_id": "image-1",
                        "oss_url": "https://example.invalid/1.jpg",
                        "asset_id": first.id,
                        "asset_sha256": first.sha256,
                    },
                    {
                        "cloud_id": "image-2",
                        "oss_url": "https://example.invalid/2.jpg",
                        "asset_id": second.id,
                        "asset_sha256": second.sha256,
                    },
                ],
            }
        }
        expected = {
            "case_cloud_id": "case-100",
            "level": "L1",
            "final_score": 88,
            "case_review": {"score": 73, "style": "现代"},
        }
        response = client.post(
            "/api/baseline-sets",
            json={
                "name": "案例结构化真值",
                "category_key": "design_case",
                "items": [
                    {
                        "asset_id": first.id,
                        "truth_contract_key": DESIGN_CASE_TRUTH_CONTRACT,
                        "expected_result": expected,
                        "workflow_input": workflow_input,
                    }
                ],
            },
        )

        assert response.status_code == 200, response.text
        item = db.scalar(select(BaselineSetItem))
        assert item is not None
        assert item.truth_contract_key == DESIGN_CASE_TRUTH_CONTRACT
        snapshot = json.loads(item.asset_snapshot_json)
        assert snapshot["workflow_input"] == workflow_input
        assert json.loads(item.expected_result_json or "{}") == expected

        mismatch = client.post(
            "/api/baseline-sets",
            json={
                "name": "案例哈希漂移",
                "category_key": "design_case",
                "items": [
                    {
                        "asset_id": first.id,
                        "truth_contract_key": DESIGN_CASE_TRUTH_CONTRACT,
                        "expected_result": expected,
                        "workflow_input": {
                            "case": {
                                **workflow_input["case"],
                                "images": [
                                    {
                                        **workflow_input["case"]["images"][0],
                                        "asset_sha256": "d" * 64,
                                    }
                                ],
                            }
                        },
                    }
                ],
            },
        )
        assert mismatch.status_code == 409
        assert mismatch.json()["detail"]["code"] == (
            "design_case_image_hash_mismatch"
        )

        invalid_result = client.post(
            "/api/baseline-sets",
            json={
                "name": "案例错误终态路径",
                "category_key": "design_case",
                "items": [
                    {
                        "asset_id": first.id,
                        "truth_contract_key": DESIGN_CASE_TRUTH_CONTRACT,
                        "expected_result": {
                            "case_cloud_id": "case-100",
                            "level": "L1",
                            "style": "错误地放在顶层",
                        },
                        "workflow_input": workflow_input,
                    }
                ],
            },
        )
        assert invalid_result.status_code == 422
        assert invalid_result.json()["detail"]["code"] == (
            "design_case_truth_result_invalid"
        )


def test_structured_truth_cannot_fall_back_to_legacy_regression_engine() -> None:
    with _structured_baseline_client() as (client, _db, asset):
        contract_key = "kg.knowledge_graph.domestic.whole.v1"
        expected = resolve_knowledge_graph_fixture(
            "kg.domestic.whole.v1",
            contract_key,
            expected_asset_id="ll-domestic-legacy-guard",
        )
        created = client.post(
            "/api/baseline-sets",
            json={
                "name": "知识图谱原生执行门禁",
                "category_key": "knowledge_graph",
                "default_expected_level": None,
                "items": [
                    {
                        "asset_id": asset.id,
                        "truth_contract_key": contract_key,
                        "expected_result": expected,
                    }
                ],
            },
        )
        assert created.status_code == 200, created.text

        response = client.post(
            f"/api/baseline-sets/{created.json()['id']}/runs",
            json={},
        )

        assert response.status_code == 422
        assert response.json()["detail"]["code"] == (
            "structured_truth_native_workflow_required"
        )


def test_structured_truth_fails_closed_for_invalid_contract_payload() -> None:

                        "truth_contract_key": (
                            "kg.knowledge_graph.domestic.whole.v1"
                        ),
